备注： 12 pages, 7 figures. Code: [this https URL](https://github.com/Ray-Tiann/AutoRSE)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Recommender Systems Engineer Agents (RSE-Agents), which use LLM-based agents to autonomously improve recommendation algorithms, are emerging as a promising paradigm. However, their capabilities and limitations remain poorly understood due to the lack of a unified, standardized evaluation framework and systematic trajectory-level analysis. To address this gap, we introduce AutoRSE, an evaluation framework spanning six representative industrial-grade recommendation tasks, and conduct a large-scale empirical study of three frontier LLMs across three widely used agent harnesses. Our results show that RSE-Agents consistently enhance the performance of backbones on five of the six tasks, with an average relative improvement of 16.1%, indicating that they can already autonomously improve recommendation performance. However, trajectory-level analysis also reveals that current RSE-Agents function primarily as engineering optimizers rather than algorithmic improvers: their gains are largely driven by hyperparameter tuning, training strategies, and model ensembling, while genuine algorithmic innovations are rare. Since the largest gains in recommender systems often stem from algorithmic advances, this behavior underscores a fundamental limitation of current RSE-Agents. To probe this limitation, we perform idea-injection experiments and show that agents equipped with innovative ideas from frontier research or human experts achieve substantially larger improvements than those relying on self-generated ideas. These results suggest that idea generation is a central bottleneck of current RSE-Agents, highlights a key direction for the future recommendation system research. Our implementation is publicly available on GitHub: this https URL.

10. 【2610.37176】Lossless Compression of Proximity Graph Indexes for Efficient Approximate Nearest Neighbors Search

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37176

作者：Francesco Benocci,Leonard Dahlmann,Francesco Gargiulo,Tobias Heuer,Silvio Martinico,Franco Maria Nardini,Cosimo Rulli,Rossano Venturini

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：Graph-based Approximate Nearest, Approximate Nearest Neighbor, Graph-based Approximate, Nearest Neighbor, Approximate Nearest

备注： 7 pages, 5 figures, Accepted at CIKM 2026. Code: [this https URL](https://github.com/TusKANNy/kannolo) . Reproducibility: [this https URL](https://github.com/tusKANNy/papers_reproducibility/tree/main/cikm2026)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Graph-based Approximate Nearest Neighbor (ANN) indexes, such as HNSW, are the backbone of modern vector search. However, their massive memory footprint creates a severe infrastructure bottleneck. While vector quantization is widely adopted to reduce the memory required to store data vectors, the graph's edge structure increasingly dominates total memory usage. Compressing these adjacency lists for online search presents a critical system challenge: because decompression occurs during query processing, i.e., while navigating the graph, memory savings cannot come at the expense of query latency. In this paper, we present a compression technique explicitly tailored for live HNSW routing. By applying offline graph permutations to artificially induce structural locality, we enable the use of SIMD-accelerated integer compressors, such as Stream VByte, for rapid on-the-fly decompression. Evaluated across diverse real-world workloads, we significantly outperform the current state of the art in both compression ratio and query latency. Our experimental results show that we achieve compression ratios up to 52.7% with negligible penalties in latency compared to the uncompressed baseline. Furthermore, our technique has been effectively deployed in production at eBay, achieving up to 44.3% reduction in edge storage with no degradation in query latency.

11. 【2610.37162】Coverage Under Contradiction: A Benchmark for Negation-Aware Retrieval

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37162

作者：Pasha Abrishamchian,Eugene Yang,Andrew Yates,Nazli Goharian,Ophir Frieder

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：Coverage-evaluation benchmarks quantify, retrieved evidence covers, Coverage-evaluation benchmarks, quantify whether retrieved, needed to satisfy

备注： 5 pages, 1 figure. Accepted at CIKM 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Coverage-evaluation benchmarks quantify whether retrieved evidence covers the different pieces of information needed to satisfy an information need. Existing benchmarks mostly consider factual claims that may already be known to pretrained retrieval components, leaving open whether retrievers find corpus evidence when it contradicts those priors. We study how retrievers behave when their parametric knowledge and the information in a corpus disagree. To do so, we develop a process to augment a nugget-based coverage benchmark with controlled contradictions. For each nugget, we create a negated counterpart and edit its supporting documents to support that negation while preserving the context. Applying this process to CoverageBench, a benchmark measuring information coverage, we create CoverageBench-Negate, spanning six datasets and roughly 63,000 original-edited evidence pairs (one per nugget-document link). We attach rich construction metadata to every pair and additionally provide out-of-family LLM judgments over all pairs and a human audit of a sample. In baseline experiments, BM25 separates original from edited near-twin evidence substantially better than dense retrievers (Qwen3 and Jina), which stay near chance. We release the benchmark on Hugging Face Datasets (nuggets, documents, pairs) together with the construction materials, including the prompts used to create it.

12. 【2610.37140】Rethinking Positional Encoding and Interaction Granularity for CTR Prediction

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37140

作者：Xinxun Zhang,Yuxiang Wang,Xiaokai Zhou,Jiayue Li,Yi Li,Yue Liu,Jun Zhang,Xiaoling Zhou,Tieyun Qian,Xiao Yan,Jiawei Jiang

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：Click-through rate, Existing Transformer-based CTR, Transformer-based CTR models, Transformer-based CTR, contextual features

备注： 13 pages, 11 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Click-through rate (CTR) prediction relies on historical behaviors and contextual features to estimate clicks on a target item. Existing Transformer-based CTR models often adopt index-based or temporal positional encodings and token-level causal interaction. However, we observe that most existing positional encodings do not outperform NoPE, while relaxing causal constraints within target-relative temporal groups also yields gains. These observations motivate a fundamental question: what positional encoding and interaction granularity does CTR prediction actually require? To address this question, we propose \textbf{TRIP}, which combines target-aware rank--time positional encoding with temporal-group interaction modeling. Specifically, Factorized Rank Encoding represents each behavior's ordinal position within the historical sequence through a low-rank factorization, while Target-Relative Time Encoding characterizes its temporal distance to the target on a coarse-to-fine scale. This joint design grounds each behavior's positional representation in its sequence context and temporal relation to the current target. Furthermore, Temporal Group-Causal Interaction enriches intra-group representations through bidirectional interaction and masked reconstruction while preserving inter-group causal order. Finally, behavior representations are condensed into compact group summaries for efficient group-level modeling in subsequent layers. Experiments on four public benchmarks show that TRIP improves AUC by up to 4.45\% relative to the strongest baseline.

13. 【2610.37080】ssera: Structure-Completed Evidence Units for Web Retrieval-Augmented Generation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37080

作者：Peichun Hua,He Sun,Zirui Zhuang,Qi Qi,Yiming Qiu,Shinan Liu,Yunming Xiao

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：Web retrieval-augmented generation, retrieved item serves, Web retrieval-augmented, recent visual pipelines, fixed-size text chunks

备注： 13 pages, 7 figures, 17 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Web retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) splits a page into fixed-size text chunks or, in recent visual pipelines, screenshot tiles, and the retrieved item serves at once as the unit of indexing, ranking, and reading. We trace answer evidence from source HTML to reader input over the full English Wikipedia and find that this coupling loses evidence in text and screenshot pipelines alike: evidence present in the source page often fails to reach the reader intact, particularly structured content such as tables and infoboxes, which chunking separates from the headers and context that give it meaning. Stronger extraction, context expansion, and larger reader budgets leave the loss in place. We present Tessera, a training-free evidence delivery layer that decouples what a pipeline retrieves from what its reader reads. Given the pages behind any ranked list, Tessera rebuilds their HTML into fine-grained evidence units, each completed with its page title, section path, and table headers, selects the units relevant to the query, and packs them into the reader context at an unchanged token budget. The index, the retriever, and the reader stay as they are, so Tessera applies to text, screenshot, and agentic pipelines. Across three question answering benchmarks, Tessera improves answer accuracy by 5 to 6 points on text and screenshot pipelines, consistently across readers, retrievers, and reader budgets, and outperforms strong HTML-aware and structure-aware RAG baselines under matched budgets. The gains carry over to multi-step agentic search. Our code is publicly available at this https URL.

14. 【2610.37005】Compote: Collapse-Resistant Multiple Hypothesis Learning For Sequential Recommendation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37005

作者：Louis Castres Saint Martin,Clement Calauzenes,Alain Rakotomamonjy,Patrick Gallinari

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：single interaction history, admits multiple plausible, multiple plausible continuations, distinct catalogue regions, inherently ambiguous

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Next-item prediction is inherently ambiguous: a single interaction history often admits multiple plausible continuations across distinct catalogue regions. Standard sequential recommenders compress these alternatives into a single prediction, conflating user intents. We introduce COllapse-resistant Multiple hyPOThesis lEarning (COMPOTE), a multi-hypothesis transformer trained with an annealed objective that transitions from uniform supervision to a winner-takes-all assignment. This mechanism forces hypotheses to specialize into distinct catalogue regions, while inference relies on an efficient, normalizer-free max-similarity rule. Theoretically, we show that in the population case, the optimal solutions partition the conditional next-item distribution according to a max-entropy quantization principle, while pooled scores preserve the Bayes-optimal ranking. We also prove a generalization bound scaling logarithmically with the number of hypotheses. Across three Amazon Reviews 2023 benchmarks, COMPOTE outperforms strong ID-based, generative, and multi-interest baselines using item IDs alone. Matched-capacity ablations confirm that performance gains stem directly from the annealed objective, preventing the head collapse observed in conventional multi-head architectures, rather than added capacity, ensembling, or routing.

15. 【2610.36991】SpectRAG: Weighted Chunk Graphs for Token-Budgeted Context Selection in Multi-Hop Retrieval-Augmented Generation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36991

作者：Zafer Sahinoglu,Abdulazeez Mohammed,Buse Yaren Kazangirler,Cahit Berkay Kazangirler

类目：Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：systems ground large, large language models, ground large language, retrieving external evidence, systems ground

备注： 26 pages, 5 figures, 10 tables. This work is intended for submission to an IEEE journal

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems ground large language models (LLMs) by retrieving external evidence and conditioning generation on the retrieved context. Although dense retrieval is effective for local semantic matching, independently ranked retrieval can return redundant passages, miss cross-chunk dependencies, and provide incomplete evidence for multi-hop reasoning. We propose SpectRAG, a spectral graph-augmented retrieval framework that combines hybrid candidate seeding, weighted chunk graph construction, graph-based expansion, spectral relevance propagation, and token-budgeted context selection. Document chunks are represented by dense semantic embeddings and graph-spectral coordinates derived from a weighted chunk graph whose edges encode semantic similarity, document adjacency, section hierarchy, shared entities, citation links, and similarity between LLM-generated utility questions. At query time, SpectRAG builds a high-recall seed set using exact inner-product search and hybrid relevance signals, expands it over the chunk graph, re-scores candidates using graph proximity and spectral information, and assembles the final context under a fixed token budget. The goal is not merely to retrieve individually similar chunks, but to construct a context that is relevant, diverse, structurally connected, and sufficient for grounded reasoning. Experiments on MultiHopRAG, UltraDomain, and long-document question-answering settings show that SpectRAG improves broad evidence recovery and achieves competitive generation quality against dense, hybrid, HyDE, LightRAG, and NaiveRAG baselines. The results further indicate that graph-spectral structure is particularly useful for inference-oriented and cross-document retrieval, while highlighting trade-offs in early precision, computational cost, and domain-dependent performance.

16. 【2610.36973】KL-MOPD: Discrepancy-Aware Multi-Objective On-Policy Distillation for Generative Recommendation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36973

作者：Weiqin Yang,Yandong Wang,Yuan Gao,Hao Deng,Jinxin Hu,Jing Cao,Jiawei Chen

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：Coordinating multiple recommendation, Coordinating multiple, click and conversion, real-world generative recommendation, critical for real-world

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Coordinating multiple recommendation objectives (e.g., click and conversion) is critical for real-world generative recommendation (GR). However, since a positive item in GR can be retrieved only when its tokens are successively selected at every decoding step, traditional item-wise methods lack fine-grained token-wise supervision to guide intermediate steps. Motivated by its success in foundation models, we consider introducing multi-objective on-policy distillation (MOPD) into GR to distill two single-objective teachers into a student at each token. Yet, we find that in vanilla MOPD, (i) teachers exhibit unequal distillation difficulty at each token (i.e., token-level imbalance), and (ii) the harder-to-distill teacher varies across tokens (i.e., cross-token inconsistency), leaving each objective poorly learned at certain steps and hindering positive item retrieval for both objectives, leading to suboptimal performance. To address this challenge, we propose KL-MOPD (KL-Weighted Multi-Objective On-Policy Distillation), a simple yet effective distillation method that allocates token-wise teacher weights proportional to the initial student-teacher token-wise KL divergences, thereby strengthening supervision for the harder-to-distill teacher at every decoding step. Despite its simplicity, we theoretically prove that KL-MOPD strictly outperforms vanilla MOPD in the additive joint recommendation objective under mild conditions. Moreover, KL-MOPD is plug-and-play and computationally efficient, requiring only a one-time pre-training weight calibration with negligible training overhead. Extensive experiments on three public benchmarks, a billion-scale industrial dataset, and online A/B tests on our e-commerce advertising platform demonstrate that KL-MOPD significantly improves both individual and joint recommendation performance. The anonymous code is provided for peer review.

17. 【2610.36958】PailitaoMMSearch2.0 Technical Report: Scaling Generative Retrieval to Unified End-to-End Multimodal Search

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36958

作者：Xiaohan Ye,Zihan Gong,Jingqian Zhao,Lianyu Du,Yunmeng Shu,Xu Chen,Zesheng Zhou,Renchun You,Bocheng Chen,Jinsong Lan,Haihong Tang,Bo Zheng

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：Generative retrieval models, industrial generative retrieval, Generative retrieval, textbf, increasingly adopted

备注： PailitaoMMSearch2.0 Technical Report

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Generative retrieval models have been increasingly adopted in search and recommendation systems. However, industrial generative retrieval models are commonly specialized to a fixed online scenario: they learn from scenario-specific interactions, support a narrow set of query forms and retrieval objectives. We study how to remove these couplings and build a unified end-to-end multimodal search system for open-ended image--text queries, heterogeneous shopping intents, and online scenarios with different resource constraints. We present \textbf{PailitaoMMSearch}, which directly generates product Semantic IDs from a raw query image and free-form instruction and scales along three dimensions. \textbf{Data scaling} uses a reusable toolset that can be composed into intent- and lifecycle-specific workflows for cold-start synthesis, quality control, real-traffic refinement, and continuous data production. \textbf{Intent scaling} progressively equips one model with e-commerce product knowledge, multiple retrieval relations, and adjustable reasoning budgets through continual pre-training (CPT), mixed-budget supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and unified reinforcement learning (RL). The RL stage combines intent-balanced optimization and Sample--Beam rollouts aligned with multi-candidate serving. \textbf{Scenario scaling} exposes quality--cost operating points across model capacity and reasoning budget. The system is deployed in multimodal search applications on Taobao Pailitao. A controlled online A/B test reports statistically significant improvements, including item page views $\textbf{+17.90\%}$, transactions $\textbf{+27.04\%}$, and gross merchandise value $\textbf{+8.94\%}$.

18. 【2610.36954】When Diffusion Recommenders Stop Sampling: Preserving Generative Diversity

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36954

作者：Louis Castres Saint Martin(ISIR, MLIA),Alain Rakotomamonjy,Patrick Gallinari(ISIR, MLIA)

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：admit multiple plausible, single deterministic ranking, plausible next interactions, conventional sequential recommenders, sequential recommenders produce

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:A user history can admit multiple plausible next interactions, yet conventional sequential recommenders produce a single deterministic ranking. While diffusion models offer a natural way to sample alternative continuations, we show that existing target-item diffusion recommenders often suffer from severe sampling collapse, producing nearly identical outputs across independent draws. We identify two ubiquitous design choices that promote this collapse: joint learning of the latent space and denoiser, and in-trajectory supervision via discrete losses. To address these failure modes, we introduce DARTS (Diverse and Accurate Recommendation Through Sampling), a three-stage latent diffusion framework that explicitly decouples representation learning, generative modeling, and discrete ranking by learning and freezing the latent space before diffusion training and postponing ranking supervision to the final decoding stage. We further characterize sampling diversity using complementary measures of variation before and after decoding, and use posterior-predictive aggregation to exploit this diversity at inference. Across three Amazon Reviews 2023 categories, DARTS preserves substantially greater sampling diversity while outperforming strong sequential, generative-retrieval, and diffusion baselines by 7-12% on standard ranking metrics. Aggregating multiple samples further improves ranking accuracy at a fixed slate size.

19. 【2610.36915】Do We Need an LLM to Know Why Sources Conflict? Decision Models for Knowledge-Conflict Typing in RAG

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36915

作者：Parv Khatri,Harshul Jain,Mehak Kapoor,Tanya Sah

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：query disagree, contested topic, stale information, decision models, disagree

备注： 15 pages, 2 figures, 8 tables. Code, prompts and all model outputs: [this https URL](https://github.com/Parv17k/decision-models-conflict-typing)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:When the sources retrieved for a query disagree, the right response depends on why they disagree: a question with several valid answers, a contested topic, stale information and a false source each call for different behaviour. Cattan et al. (2025) showed that telling a generator the conflict type improves its responses by about 24 points, but that predicting the type is hard even for frontier LLMs. We study whether typed decision models, which return probabilities over fixed options in a single forward pass instead of generating text, can do this classification fast and cheaply. Using the CONFLICTS benchmark and the original paper's prompt verbatim, we evaluate all 11 decision models served by one commercial inference gateway, from 7 providers, against Gemini models on identical inputs. Asked directly, the decision models reach 50.0--67.0% accuracy; 10 of the 11 match or exceed Gemini 2.5 Flash, the paper's model (61.3%), at 4--39x lower billed cost (2 were free) and 2.6--6.0x lower latency. The current Gemini 3.8 Flash (70.0%) is significantly more accurate than 8 of them, but sending each decision model's least confident quarter of queries to it gives a cascade statistically tied with it for 8 of the 11, at 25--37% of its cost. End to end, answers generated with the types of 10 of the 11 follow the expected behaviour as often as with the LLM's types. Decomposing the taxonomy into yes/no questions lowers accuracy for every model, and a repair fitted on one model does not transfer. Pre-specified diagnostics show that dates play no measurable role and that sensitivity to source order varies widely between models. Decision models detect a planted false source less reliably than the LLM as hard labels, but their probabilities still separate planted from clean sources. We release code, prompts and all model outputs at this https URL.

20. 【2610.36890】Latent Residual Reasoning for Ranking in Generative Recommendation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36890

作者：Haihan Shi,Ruidong Han,Wenjie Wang,Shangyu Chen,Sunhao Dai,Yongqi Li,Yangyang Li,Fei Jiang,Wei Lin,Fuli Feng,Xiangnan He

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：ranking typically adopt, strong practical efficiency, reasoning, typically adopt, adopt a fast-thinking

备注： Accepted by CIKM 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Generative recommendation (GR) models for ranking typically adopt a fast-thinking direct prediction paradigm with strong practical efficiency. However, ranking requires fine-grained scoring across multiple candidate items, while existing GR models still mainly rely on one-shot prediction. This raises the question whether additional inference-time computation can further improve ranking quality. Recently, inspired by Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning in large language models (LLMs), some work has begun to introduce reasoning into recommendation systems. Some of these methods rely on LLM reasoning frameworks to perform explicit reasoning in textual space, but such designs are not suitable for more widely deployed non-textual recommendation models. A more relevant direction is to introduce latent reasoning into ID-based recommendation models. Although recent work has extended latent reasoning to industrial ranking, how successive reasoning steps should improve fine-grained candidate scores remains less explored. To address this problem, we propose Refiner, a latent residual reasoning framework with parameter-efficient fine-tuning for generative ranking. Our approach introduces three key components: (1) a residual reasoning mechanism that progressively refines ranking scores through cumulative additive corrections, (2) history queries that provide condensed user behavior representations to enhance candidate-specific reasoning, and (3) a parameter-efficient fine-tuning strategy that leverages LoRA modules to adapt pre-trained models for latent reasoning. Extensive experiments on two public benchmarks, an offline industrial dataset, and an online A/B test demonstrate that Refiner consistently improves over direct prediction, validating its effectiveness and practical utility.

21. 【2610.36836】DuTri: Dual-Branch Modeling with Triadic Factorization for Multimodal Multi-Scenario Recommendation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36836

作者：Jintao Huang,Chaohua Yang,Kailiang Hao,Dugang Liu,Xing Tang,Zhong Ming

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：multimodal information grows, grows more prevalent, recommender systems, multimodal information, multimodal

备注： 12 pages, 7 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:As recommendation scenarios become increasingly diverse and multimodal information grows more prevalent, effectively modeling cross-scenario user preferences and multimodal semantics has become a critical challenge in recommender systems. When incorporating multimodal information, existing multi-scenario recommendation (MSR) methods typically treat textual and visual representations as generic input features and directly feed them into their original parameter-sharing architectures. However, this extension may not fully accommodate the different modeling requirements of collaborative and multimodal information and may not be the best modeling paradigm. Our controlled experiments show that directly adopting these architectures is not the optimal modeling approach. To this end, we propose Dual-Branch Modeling with Triadic Factorization (DuTri), a framework for multimodal multi-scenario recommendation comprising a collaborative branch and a multimodal branch. Specifically, the collaborative branch learns structured collaborative preference representations through CANDECOMP/PARAFAC (CP) decomposition of user-scenario-item interactions and residual quantization, thereby capturing cross-scenario commonalities and scenario-specific differences. The multimodal branch leverages the learned user and scenario preferences to guide adaptive modality fusion; additionally, item-level contrastive learning aligns collaborative item preference representations with multimodal representations to facilitate effective collaboration between the two branches. Extensive experiments on two real-world datasets, AntM$^2$C and Amazon, demonstrate that DuTri outperforms existing multimodal and multi-scenario recommendation baselines in overall recommendation performance, validating the effectiveness of the proposed framework.

22. 【2610.36763】he Vocabulary Is Already Here: Adapting Pretrained Interpretable Features for Generative Recommendation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36763

作者：Ziluowen Luo,Ruochen Liu,Jun Yin,Hao Yan,Zhengxuan Hu,Xinze Shi,Chaozhuo Li,Chengqi Zhang,Senzhang Wang

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：Generative recommendation, SID, Generative, item, commonly fit SID

备注： 13 pages, 8 figures, 7 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Generative recommendation (GR) predicts the next item through its semantic ID (SID). Existing methods commonly fit SID vocabularies to pretrained language model (PLM) representations of item descriptions, incurring additional training costs and making vocabulary coverage dependent on the fitting domains. We observe that pretrained sparse autoencoders expose PLM features with human-interpretable semantic annotations, allowing feature indices to serve as a reusable SID vocabulary. However, reusing this vocabulary requires preserving item distinctions under compact token budgets and learning to favor target items over candidates with shared dominant features. It also requires extending feature interpretability from item content to retrieval decisions. To address these challenges, we propose API, a framework that Adapts the Pretrained Interpretable vocabulary for effective and explainable GR without additional vocabulary learning. Within API, Pretrained Interpretable IDs (PIDs) retain selected feature indices and relative activation strengths for item discrimination. With PIDs as supervision, the Semantic Prediction Adapter (SPA) learns user preferences from interaction histories through joint feature-index and strength prediction. SPA combines both predictions to score candidate items, with item-level supervision encouraging the observed next item to outrank its alternatives. API further explains candidate score differences through feature contributions paired with their pretrained semantic annotations. Extensive experiments show that API achieves competitive performance across backbones and decoding schemes, while preserving item distinctions under compact token budgets and enabling explainable retrieval.

23. 【2610.36585】Recall or Relearn? A Mixture of In-Context Experts for Recurring Concept Drift

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36585

作者：Zhangkai Wu,Xu Zhang,Zhihong Cui,Yiqi Wang,Yi Zhang

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：Tabular foundation models, Web data streams, network intrusion detection, Tabular foundation, applied to Web

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Tabular foundation models (TFMs) are being applied to Web data streams such as network intrusion detection, where they adapt by updating the labelled rows of their context without retraining. When a past concept returns after leaving the context, a TFM faces a recall-or-relearn decision between restoring stored rows of the concept and relearning it from new labels. We propose MICE (Mixture of In-Context Experts), a training-free learner that cuts the stream into segments at detected changes. It merges a closed segment into a stored context when the TFM's estimated additional error on the segment is within a tolerance, under a capacity limit on stored contexts. A fast level weights the stored contexts and first-in-first-out (FIFO) windows by the log-loss of their predictions on the last labelled batch. We show that, with pure stored contexts and a margin condition, this fast level gains over relearning, in expected accuracy, the normalised positive area between the stored context's accuracy and the TFM's learning curve, up to a first-batch term. A slow level mixes the fast level's prediction with that of the FIFO window of the last labelled rows and bounds the discounted half-Brier regret of MICE against this window at every time. Learning curves measured beforehand predict the ordering of the observed recall gains. MICE outperforms reset, which empties the context at detected drifts, on grid streams with random boundary phases. Merging keeps fewer experts than pools that store every segment, and with fixed segmentation a time-matched snapshot pool matches MICE on the shifted grid. On real streams, MICE has the highest mean accuracy of all policies we compare.

Abstract:Deploying conversational assistants at government service desks faces a tension between regulatory certainty (zero tolerance for invented fees), data sovereignty prohibiting commercial clouds, and high rates of underspecified or out-of-jurisdiction citizen queries. We present and evaluate a governed, sovereign Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) architecture deployed on the official portal of the State of Tlaxcala, Mexico (this http URL), covering 1,143 procedures and a decoupled directory of 924 offices. The system governs interactions via three pillars: (1) a two-stage tripartite epistemic gate (clarify_policy and a Boltzmann distribution over AnyJev) arbitrating among answering (SPECIFIC), facet-guided clarification (CLARIFY), and abstaining (ABSTAIN); (2) a post-generation deterministic numeric verifier (verify_numeric_guardrail) with SHA-256 cache invalidation ensuring every generated fee or validity figure belongs to the retrieved official record; and (3) an empirical evaluation across 380 organic production traces (8 weeks) and a 200-query stratified benchmark (5-fold CV: 86.41% +/- 7.50%). 25. 【2610.36559】CCP-DP: Cross-cluster context-guided phase dynamic programming for DNA storage sequence reconstruction

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36559

作者：Zixin Zhang,Guanjin Qu,Huaming Wu

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：data storage offers, storage offers high, DNA data storage, offers high information, high information density

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Motivation: DNA data storage offers high information density and long-term stability, but accurate sequence reconstruction remains challenging under limited sequencing coverage and strong insertion, deletion, and substitution (IDS) noise. Existing reconstruction methods mainly rely on local evidence within individual read clusters. When sequencing coverage is low or indel-induced phase shifts become pronounced, such local evidence may be insufficient to distinguish closely competing candidate sequences. Results: We present Cross-Cluster Context-Guided Phase Dynamic Programming (CCP-DP), which uses dataset-level cross-cluster information to complement cluster-local evidence and selectively refines ambiguous provisional consensuses. Under the main benchmark settings, CCP-DP achieved exact reconstruction rates of 95.36%-99.98% and outperformed existing methods on multiple datasets. At $5\times$ coverage, CCP-DP improved exact reconstruction by 6.2-17.5 percentage points over the stronger of BBS and CPL across the tested IDS conditions, while maintaining low reconstruction error and computational overhead. Overall, CCP-DP introduces cross-cluster shared information as a complementary source of evidence for DNA sequence reconstruction under challenging conditions. Availability: Source code is available at this https URL.

26. 【2610.36557】QESChunker: A Single Objective Unifies Overlapping and Non-Overlapping Chunking for RAG

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36557

作者：Yifan Zhao,Qianyang Zhao,Xinyu Guan,kai wei,Yuming Deng

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Optimization and Control (math.OC)

关键词：fundamental preprocessing step, chunkers rely primarily, existing chunkers rely, retrieval-augmented generation, primarily on heuristics

备注： 16 pages, 8 figures, 4 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Chunking is a fundamental preprocessing step in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), yet most existing chunkers rely primarily on heuristics. We introduce the Question--Evidence-Supervised Chunker (QESChunker), to our knowledge the first method for constructing static chunk indices from question--evidence supervision. QESChunker constructs document-specific chunk indices by optimizing over human-annotated or LLM-generated question--evidence pairs, without access to held-out test queries. We formulate non-overlapping and overlapping index construction through a unified question--evidence utility-maximization objective, turning chunking into a supervised combinatorial optimization problem. Under the non-overlap constraint, the objective decomposes over chunks and can be optimized exactly in polynomial time by dynamic programming. When overlap is allowed, the objective becomes monotone submodular; exact optimization is NP-hard, but our greedy achieves a $(1-1/\sqrt{e})$-approximation. Across two self-constructed and three public datasets, both variants outperform existing chunking methods in complete-evidence recall in nearly all evaluated settings, under the same overlap regime and a fixed hybrid retriever. These results recast chunking as supervised, budget-aware index construction for evidence retrieval.

27. 【2610.36544】Monitor Accuracy Isn't the Answer: A Decision-Value Framework for Evaluating AI Agent Restart Monitors

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36544

作者：Siddharth Jain,Venkat N. Vedam

类目：Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：agent improves success, true-positive and false-positive, determine whether restarting, improves success, false-positive rates

备注： 29 pages, 6 figures, 14 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Classification accuracy and true-positive and false-positive rates alone do not determine whether restarting an AI agent improves success. Using an exact alarm-to-action identity for an independent clean restart with zero monitoring overhead, we show analytically that a monitor with fixed 90% accuracy can swing from a five-percentage-point loss to a 35-point gain in success rate, depending solely on the replacement attempt's residual-budget success probability. We introduce two techniques for evaluating monitors and progress signals in decision terms. First, a decision-sufficiency criterion, a binary-action specialization of Blackwell's comparison of experiments, identifies which progress representations or context compressions preserve the information needed to choose optimally between the specified actions, independent of trace reconstruction fidelity. Second, a finite-sample, model-free decision audit uses simultaneous binomial intervals over existing agent traces, without additional model calls, to certify a preference for "restart" or "continue" under stated sampling assumptions, or return "abstain" when evidence is insufficient. In a study of 194 externally evaluated model executions on 19 selected SymPy issues from SWE-bench Verified, with a retrospective audit of 130 of these traces, we find a 2.29-point apparent restart gain traced to a single surviving failed attempt, a 42.29-point loss on a second model, no positive replication of the initial gain on prospective task cohorts, and zero of 103 live comparisons meeting the specified certification bar. These results motivate evaluating agent monitors through recovery opportunity, decision value, and statistical support, rather than classification performance alone.

28. 【2610.36500】ACOR: incremental evidence accumulation towards real-time DNA storage reconstruction

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36500

作者：Linxuan Han,Huaming Wu,Shi Jia,Guanjin Qu

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR); Genomics (q-bio.GN)

关键词：DNA offers high-density, encoding digital information, DNA offers, encoding digital, digital information

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:DNA offers high-density, durable storage by encoding digital information into synthetic strands and retrieving it through sequencing. Sequence reconstruction combines noisy copies of each strand before downstream decoding. Conventional offline methods typically reconstruct completed read clusters, separating reconstruction from ongoing read collection. Streaming reconstruction offers a way to overlap evidence processing with sequencing and reduce the computation remaining before downstream decoding. We present ACOR, a streaming consensus reconstruction method that records $k$-mer transitions and boundary support in a persistent ledger, then decodes through length-constrained beam search and priority-based selection at cluster closure. Evaluation on real and simulated DNA-storage datasets showed competitive recovery. On our own short-read next-generation sequencing (NGS) wet-lab library, ACOR combined near-complete recovery with second-scale reconstruction of more than ten million reads. A smaller public benchmark was completed in a fraction of a second. Parallel ACOR achieved speedups of tens to over a thousand-fold relative to the tested serial baseline configurations. Sparse storage reduced high-context memory requirements, while parallel execution accelerated reconstruction computation. ACOR is designed for streaming readout pipelines that process assigned reads while sequencing continues.

29. 【2610.36408】Cross-Day Compatibility of Daily Item Embeddings: An Empirical Study of Alignment, Scale Management, and CTR Adaptation

Abstract:Daily item co-occurrence embeddings are paired with next-day behavior without future-behavior leakage. Third, CTR learning through a dimensionality-reducing encoder further adapts to residual drift. Against dimension-matched random-projection, SVD, and untrained-network controls, trained encoders attain the highest cross-day cosine for four of six upstream methods. The other two rank third and second in train-to-test cosine. Abstract:Evaluating natural language explanations in recommender systems is essential for fostering user trust, transparency, and engagement. To address these challenges, we propose MAREval, a structured multi-agent framework for evaluating recommendation explanations using large language models. 31. 【2610.36383】From Generation to Scoring: Scaling LLM-Based Semantic Labeling at LinkedIn

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36383

作者：Jian Sheng,Sriram Vasudevan,Sundara Raman Ramachandran,Yang Hu,Junyu Zhou,Xiaojing Ma,Haolan Qin,Tiffany Zhou,Vedant Vipul Jhaveri,Ashwini Bhingare,Chinmay Kini,Qing Lan,Xiaoqing Wang,Suleyman Cetintas,Fedor Borisyuk

关键词：industrial large language, large language model, industrial large, large language, LLM

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:For efficiency, industrial large language model (LLM) labelers often pack many candidate labels into one prompt and generate a YES/NO answer for each, even when each decision depends only on the entity and one candidate. Generating these independent decisions as one answer list couples them, spends decoding on the list, and relies on the list being well-formed. In this paper, we present an end-to-end recipe that reformulates the labeler as semantic scoring, fine-tuning it on single-candidate prompts to return each decision as one answer-token score from a prefill-only pass. We also adapt Multiple-Item Scoring (MIS) to score hundreds of candidates in one sequence with attention masked between them. Deployed for profile qualification labeling at LinkedIn, semantic scoring changes at most 0.01% of decisions when candidates are reordered and matches the agreement with reference labels that packed generation reaches only with about 4.7x as many training pairs. The MIS-based stack raises throughput over the deployed stack by 1.24x offline and 1.9x in model serving under a one-second p99 latency target. For offline batch inference, we implement MIS-based scoring as a reusable, model-agnostic Ray Data API proposed for upstream adoption. Model serving uses MIS support contributed to SGLang.

32. 【2610.36267】Preserving rare-item recall under a storage budget: quantization, distillation, and the limits of teacher fidelity

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36267

作者：Han Chen,Yingrui Li

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：Amazon Video Games, fixed embedding-storage budget, Video Games, Amazon Video, tail recall

备注： 23 pages, 2 figures, 8 tables. Supplementary data and code included as ancillary files

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Under a fixed embedding-storage budget, recommenders can use a small full-precision model, a distilled student, or a higher-rank low-precision model. We asked which choice preserves rare-item recall and whether teacher fidelity reflects it in seven dataset-backbone cells: matrix factorization on Gowalla, this http URL, Amazon Video Games, Steam, Goodbooks-10k and MovieLens-20M, and LightGCN on Amazon Video Games. Seven candidates fit in 168 bytes per user or item row: 40-dimensional Bayesian personalized ranking (BPR), two 40-dimensional unbiased knowledge distillation (UnKD) students, and factorizations of a 300-dimensional teacher's scores at ranks 40, 80, 155 and 300, stored in 32, 16, 8 and 4 bits. Each test set was evaluated once after fixing candidates and confirmatory analyses. The rank-300 4-bit factorization used 13% of teacher storage and retained 95-103% of its tail recall@20 and 93-97% of its NDCG@20 in every cell. The rank-40 factorization had the lowest tail recall in every cell; the 4-bit factorization had 1.2-12.7 times its tail recall and 0.99-1.46 times its NDCG. UnKD students retained 56-102% of teacher tail recall. In all ten prespecified comparisons across five prospective-expansion cells, the 4-bit factorization had higher tail recall, with simultaneous 95% intervals excluding zero. Teacher-hit retention, the share of the teacher's relevant tail hits also found by a model, ordered 23 of 147 candidate pairs differently from tail recall and identified the wrong tail-recall leader in 3 of 7 cells: models can find relevant tail items the teacher misses. In these cells, spending bytes on rank rather than precision preserved rare-item recall at 7.5 times the arithmetic per score. Compressed recommenders should be judged by total tail recall, not teacher fidelity alone.

33. 【2610.36239】BehaveRec: When Does Long-Term Behavioral History Help Sequential Recommendation? A Study with Session, History, and Community Signals

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36239

作者：Aysenur Inan,Reza Yousefi Maragheh,Jayesh Uddhav Kudase,Selene Xu,Kaushiki Nag,Jianpeng Xu,Sushant Kumar

类目：Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：Sequential recommendation models, Sequential recommendation, recommendation models typically, models typically predict, recent session interactions

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Sequential recommendation models typically predict a user's next action from recent session interactions, which limits personalization when short-term signals are weak or ambiguous. Rather than assuming that incorporating longer-term history and community signals is universally beneficial, we study when it actually helps. We propose BehaveRec, a three-signal framework combining a causal session transformer, a bidirectional User History Encoder over a 90-day window, and a GraphSAGE module over a user-user graph built from behavioral embedding similarity rather than item co-occurrence; a learned gate blends the three signals per user. On an industrial dataset of 137M sequences from 12.6M users, BehaveRec improves R@5 by +3.2% over SASRec, with a larger-capacity SASRec baseline (more parameters than BehaveRec) still trailing by +2.5%, ruling out capacity as the explanation. More importantly, a user-level stratification by history length reveals a clear dose-response relationship: BehaveRec's advantage is negligible below roughly 10 interactions and grows monotonically with history richness, reaching +0.017 R@5 for users with 101+ events. This pattern is corroborated across one industrial and three public datasets spanning history-rich to history-sparse regimes, where session-only models remain preferable. These headline results, and the resulting deployment threshold, are established for category-level intent prediction; a preliminary, single-run check on the full item-level vocabulary yields a directionally mixed result and is scoped as an open question rather than a claim. We use these results to derive a practical deployment rule -- roughly 20 interactions per user -- for when long-term history and behavioral-graph signals are worth their added complexity. Despite this added complexity, BehaveRec remains lightweight: it uses 5.67M parameters and trains on a single GPU in under two days.

34. 【2610.36208】Counterfactual Profile Perturbations for Recommendation Explanations: A Reproducibility Study

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36208

作者：Meysam Varasteh,Elizabeth McKinnie,Robin Burke

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：Learning to eXplain, reproducibility paper explores, counterfactual explanation technique, LXR, eXplain Recommendations

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:This reproducibility paper explores the counterfactual explanation technique known as Learning to eXplain Recommendations (LXR). Prior work has shown that LXR can reliably identify perturbations that, if applied to a user's profile, cause the user's top ranked item to no longer be recommended, thereby explaining the item's recommendation as a function of the user's rating choices. In this paper, we extend this work in multiple ways. We expand the scope of the original LXR study to look at explanations for items other than the most highly recommended item, finding that LXR is also effective at this version of the task. We consider alternate baselines for LXR, including item popularity and latent factor similarity, and we see that for some tasks these alternatives work surprisingly well. We also examine the impact of item popularity, finding that LXR offers better explanation quality (shorter perturbations) when less popular items are being explained. Finally, we conduct a brute-force exploration of a subset of the perturbation space in order to estimate a lower bound for optimal perturbation size against which LXR and other such methods can be compared.

35. 【2610.36181】Is the education gap in generative AI use smaller than the internet's was at a similar level of use? Evidence from 16 European countries

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36181

作者：Keisuke Watanabe

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR); Computers and Society (cs.CY)

备注： 16 pages, 2 figures. Code, data and outputs are provided as ancillary files

点击查看摘要

Abstract:New technologies are usually adopted first by people with more formal education. This paper asks whether the education gap in generative AI use in 2025 is smaller or larger than the internet's gap in the same country in the year when overall internet use was at a similar level. It uses public Eurostat survey data on ICT usage by individuals aged 16 to 74. Sixteen of 35 countries could be matched; most of the others were early internet adopters. The point-gap result keeps its sign in every generative-AI-versus-internet sensitivity analysis with 8 or more countries, including comparisons restricted on both sides to ages 25 to 54 and 55 to 74, but its size is sensitive to matching that takes education composition into account (about -10 to -11 points in 7 and 8 countries). The ratio result is fragile: it fails the decision rule when countries matched at their first internet observation are removed (9 of 11, p = 0.065). The analysis follows an unpublished plan whose timing cannot be verified, so it is not presented as pre-registered. The results describe unadjusted education contrasts in a non-random set of countries, not an education effect, and do not generalise to the EU as a whole.

36. 【2610.36134】When Connectivity Misleads: Guiding Agentic Search with Contrastive Evidence Paths

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36134

作者：Junchen Li,Liang Xu,Rongzheng Wang,Qizhi Chen,Junhao Wen,Shuang Liang

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：choose subsequent actions, subsequent actions based, limited observation window, Search agents, choose subsequent

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Search agents can use cross-document graphs to discover sources through multi-hop connections and choose subsequent actions based on candidate rankings and relationships within a limited observation window. However, documents may rank highly because their numerous connections make them easier to reach, without necessarily providing more valuable evidence. Graph diffusion methods such as Personalized PageRank (PPR) aggregate propagation contributions from multiple sources, allowing highly connected distractor nodes to crowd the observation window. Flat node scores also obscure cross-document evidence trails, limiting continued exploration. We propose Contrastive Evidence Paths (CEP), which scores candidates by their strongest individual paths on a query-conditioned document graph and calibrates these scores against the same graph's background visitation distribution to mitigate structural bias. CEP ranks candidates by the calibrated scores and retains the corresponding source paths. Building on CEP, we develop Structured Corpus Interaction (SCI), which organizes candidates and their connecting paths into a structured Map, enabling agents to verify source documents and pursue missing information through expansion from anchor documents. Experiments on BrowseComp-Plus and TREC-COVID show that SCI achieves state-of-the-art answer accuracy and nDCG@10 with the lowest generation cost. Compared with the strongest baselines by task performance, maximum reductions in tool calls and total generation costs exceed 50% and 40%, respectively. Further analysis confirms that SCI with CEP suppresses ranking inflation induced by structural connections and improves gold recall and exploration efficiency.

37. 【2610.36000】RELAY: One Query, Two Roles for Sequential Recommendation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36000

作者：Zesheng Li,Chengchang Pan,Honggang Qi

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：low inference cost, deliver accurate rankings, combine semantic preferences, recommendation must combine, associations to deliver

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Sequential recommendation must combine semantic preferences with behavioral associations to deliver accurate rankings at low inference cost. Sharing history selection connects preference representation directly to the behavioral evidence used in candidate scoring. We present RELAY, a compact sequential recommender built around one query, two roles: semantic readout and behavioral-memory routing. A single learned query forms a semantic preference representation and reuses its attention to route direct-history and transition evidence into candidate scores. 1. 【2610.37668】Honeycomb-Surfels: Compact Hexagonal Primitives for Efficient Scene Rendering and Reconstruction

Hexels push the Pareto frontier on the quality-efficiency trade-off of surface-aligned primitives.

2. 【2610.37666】hinkWorld: World Imagination Reinforces Chain-of-Dynamics Reasoning

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37666

作者：Chaehyun Kim,Chi-Pin Huang,Jisoo Kim,Min-Hung Chen,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Seungryong Kim,Fu-En Yang

备注： Project Page: [this https URL](https://thinkworld-project.github.io/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have made substantial progress in robotic manipulation, but their generalization remains constrained by the diversity and coverage of action-labeled robot demonstrations. Human videos provide much broader behavioral and environmental experience, while learning from them faces a fundamental trade-off between scalable data and explicit action grounding. We introduce ThinkWorld, a VLA learning framework that grounds action reasoning in world dynamics and strengthens it through reinforcement learning with world imagination. ThinkWorld represents each action thought as a Chain-of-Dynamics (CoD), a sequence of discrete motion tokens that captures the intermediate dynamics connecting the current world to a future state and serves as a common action representation across embodiments. We first initialize CoD with action-labeled demonstrations, then predict the future induced by each CoD and reinforce those whose predictions better match the observed outcome. This enables videos without action annotations to provide direct supervision from observed state transitions, allowing human and robot experience to refine the same reasoning space. Experiments in simulation and on real robots demonstrate effective human-to-robot transfer and strong generalization to unseen tasks and environments.

3. 【2610.37662】Can Discrete Tokens Serve as Semantically Lossless Representations of Visual Signals?

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37662

作者：Jialv Zou,Xiaotong Li,Zhengrong Yue,Xu Huang,Yaxin Luo,Wenhao Yan,Haodong Li,Wenyu Liu,Xinggang Wang

备注： Project page: [this https URL](https://doctor-james.github.io/FVQ-Page/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Discrete visual tokens represent vision in a discrete form analogous to language, offering a native interface for multimodal modeling under a shared next-token prediction objective. However, quantization error is widely assumed to cause unavoidable semantic loss, fundamentally limiting the semantic fidelity of discrete visual tokens and positioning continuous visual representations as an intrinsic upper bound. Using multimodal understanding as a proxy for semantic fidelity, we revisit this assumption and find quantization error unreliable, as discrete representations with substantially larger errors can achieve better understanding. Instead, the effective bit budget of discrete indices emerges as a key determinant of downstream performance. However, existing quantizers eventually saturate as additional codebooks contribute progressively less semantic information. As one approach to alleviating this saturation, we introduce Factorized Vector Quantization, which quantizes complementary, fixed-dimensional views of the visual feature with symmetric parallel codebooks. It continues to improve at larger bit budgets, ultimately matching continuous visual representations in multimodal understanding. Trained on industrial-scale multimodal data, our model achieves state-of-the-art performance among discrete-representation models, outperforming UniAR and Emu3-Chat by 22.8% and 59.2%, respectively, on a nine-metric average. To our knowledge, it is also the first discrete-representation model competitive with strong continuous-representation models such as Qwen3-VL, including on fine-grained OCR tasks. We further identify the key principles and a practical recipe enabling this competitiveness. These results and insights support the potential of discrete visual representations to underpin large-scale native multimodal foundation models. Code and models are available at this https URL

Abstract:Cross-view object correspondence identifies the same object across views. Our results demonstrate the effectiveness of XTrack as a unified framework, ensuring robust temporal consistency and identity preservation.

Abstract:Visual generation requires both high-level semantic knowledge and fine-grained pixel details. Within this framework, we study the objective, representation, and scaling of knowledge acquisition and rendering. Controlled comparisons identify autoregression as the stronger pretraining objective for downstream rendering, improving both learning efficiency and final generation quality. We further find that AR-only generation quality does not necessarily correlate with final performance; semantic quality matters more. This motivates a purely semantic tokenizer trained with language supervision alone, without an image decoder or reconstruction loss. We establish compute-optimal scaling laws for knowledge acquisition and rendering, and find that autoregressive pretraining lowers the fitted asymptotic rendering loss compared with pixel diffusion models trained from scratch. Guided by the laws, we train a 3B-parameter text-to-image model with only 6,600 H100 GPU-hours, achieving 86 on GenEval and 87 on DPG-Bench and validating the proposed paradigm at scale.

6. 【2610.37619】Perceiving and Predicting Dynamic Sports: Spatiotemporal Intelligence in Vision Models for Egocentric Tennis Video

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37619

作者：Tudor Braicu,Joseph DelPreto,Jared Boyer,Mónika Farsang,Maximilian Stölzle,Tim Niklas Seyde,Daniela Rus

点击查看摘要

Finally, we propose a conditional diffusion model to generate whole-body kinematic tennis motion from vision encoder predictions on egocentric video at real-time rates, adapting movements as predictions are refined. These results move towards a deeper understanding of how foundation vision models can support real-time dynamic robots using egocentric video.

7. 【2610.37613】VideoLVR: Latent Visual Reasoning over Grounded Video Evidence

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37613

作者：Shilong Li,Xingyuan Bu,Hao Shi,Yun Xing,Weixin Feng,Qiangpeng Yang,Shilei Wen

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：large language models, multimodal large language, visual, visual evidence, evidence

备注： 13 pages

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Chain-of-thought reasoning has substantially advanced video understanding in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, language-based reasoning traces do not provide a reliable mechanism for models to revisit relevant visual evidence, leading to visual forgetting and impairing their ability to reason over video content. Existing approaches maintain visual grounding during reasoning in two main ways: they either refer to evidence using textual coordinates, which specify symbolic locations rather than the visual content itself, or reinsert cropped regions or frames into the multimodal context, preserving visual details at the cost of substantial context expansion. To address these limitations, we introduce VideoLVR, which constructs latent visual evidence from local regions at relevant video moments, thereby providing visual support for intermediate reasoning steps. To supervise latent visual evidence generation, we introduce a visual token prediction objective that formulates visual token reconstruction as prediction over a vocabulary derived from the input video, thereby unifying visual evidence reconstruction and text generation within the same token prediction framework. This shared prediction framework enables MLLMs to reason over interleaved text tokens and latent visual states while integrating evidence from different moments. We further extend VideoLVR to reinforcement learning by treating sampled visual tokens as actions alongside text tokens and jointly optimizing the policy using final-answer rewards. By efficiently revisiting task-relevant visual information during reasoning, VideoLVR improves video understanding without substantially expanding the multimodal context. Experiments on multiple video benchmarks demonstrate its effectiveness, especially on tasks requiring fine-grained visual evidence.

11. 【2610.37576】he Noise Plane: A Diffusion Bridge Across Exponent and Dispersion

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37576

作者：Xuran Hu,Yuru Jia,Tengxi Wang,Miloš Daković,Ljubiša Stanković,Wufan Zhao

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Diffusion-based denoising commonly, signal-dependent statistical laws, Diffusion-based denoising, diverse signal-dependent statistical, statistical laws

备注： 23 pages, 15 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Diffusion-based denoising commonly uses Gaussian perturbations, whereas sensor noise follows diverse signal-dependent statistical laws. In this paper, we propose $p$-Bridge, a unified measurement-indexed diffusion framework for Tweedie noise. The noise exponent $p$ describes the variance--intensity relation, while accumulated measurement $\nu$ controls the noise level. Together, they define a noise plane that includes Gaussian, Poisson, and Gamma models. The stochastic bridge connects measurement distributions through independent increments, giving denoising a physical interpretation as progressive measurement accumulation. Both the starting and stopping indices can be specified, allowing restoration from an observed measurement level to a prescribed higher level. Measurement superposition yields a shared reverse-mean update, paired with a deviance-based objective for learning the required conditional mean. A latent compound Poisson--Gamma construction enables sampling at intermediate exponents. A single exponent-conditioned network adapts to different Tweedie noise models without separate training for each exponent. Experiments show that exponent conditioning improves cross-exponent robustness over fixed-exponent models. Using estimated exponents, the shared network achieves robustness comparable to an oracle-selected set of five specialist models. Experiments on synthetic images, FMD fluorescence microscopy, and multi-sensor SAR images further demonstrate its effectiveness across different signal-dependent noise regimes.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Face swapping transfers a source identity onto a target image while preserving the target's pose, expression, hair, and lighting. The most realistic current methods are diffusion-based, but they need many network evaluations per image. GAN-based methods are fast, yet trained from scratch they must learn realism from the adversarial signal alone and produce visibly weaker results. We present ALADDIN (Adversarial Latent Diffusion Distillation for One-Step Identity Swapping), a one-step face swapper obtained by initialising the generator from a pre-trained multi-step flow-matching model and training it adversarially under identity and target-preservation constraints. We additionally identify a bias in the standard evaluation protocol, which favours methods trained against the same identity encoder used to score them, and re-evaluate all baselines with an independent one. ALADDIN is state-of-the-art among one-step methods in realism and target preservation at comparable identity transfer, and matches the best multi-step model with 28 times fewer network evaluations and an order of magnitude fewer training pairs.

Abstract:Fine-grained visual perception remains a bottleneck during post-training of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), as perceptual errors can constrain gains from reasoning-focused optimization. Perception Agentic RL addresses fine-grained and dense cases unresolved by single-turn Perception RL through multi-turn inspection with visual tools. Coordinate Injection corrects spatial operations in cold-start trajectories, while a grounding-aware process reward supervises intermediate spatial fidelity alongside final-answer correctness. Training proceeds through shared cold-start SFT, single-turn Perception RL, and multi-turn Perception Agentic RL. Abstract:Visual world models increasingly support spatial reasoning by generating RGB views of unobserved states. 20. 【2610.37430】When Visual Evidence Is Insufficient: How Instructions, Post-Training, and Internal Activations Shape Refusal in Multimodal Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37430

作者：Ananth K Suresh,Aveen Dayal,Gouthaman KV,Aditya Pola,Vineeth N Balasubramanian

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：multimodal large language, withhold unsupported answers, reliable deployment, multimodal large, refusal

备注： 31 pages, 6 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:For reliable deployment, multimodal large language models (MLLMs) should recognize when an image provides insufficient evidence to answer a question and withhold unsupported answers. Yet refusal alone is not necessarily evidence of such recognition: models may refuse because of unrelated safety constraints or without identifying the missing evidence. We study how refusal on visually unanswerable questions is shaped by three sources of control: inference-time instructions, post-training, and internal activation interventions. Across three MLLMs on the MoHoBench benchmark, we systematically manipulate the meaning and placement of refusal instructions, compare supervised and preference-based post-training, and intervene on a refusal-related activation direction derived from naturally answered and refused examples. We find that refusal is highly sensitive to both the meaning and placement of instructions, with model-specific changes at the sample level that are obscured by aggregate refusal rates. Evidence-aware and general refusal instructions have stronger effects than an intentionally mismatched safety instruction. Post-training produces larger increases in refusal, but can also induce responses that combine refusal language with attempted answers. In contrast, activation intervention produces smaller yet transferable changes across prompt conditions, without evidence of indiscriminate refusal on tested visually answerable samples. These findings show that refusal is jointly shaped by external instructions, post-training, and internal representations, highlighting the need to disentangle evidence recognition from a model's learned propensity to refuse.

21. 【2610.37406】CTBakeGS: training-free baking of photorealistic CT volume renderings into 3D Gaussian splats

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37406

备注： Code and project page: [this https URL](https://github.com/BocchiSlice/CTBakeGS)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Photorealistic volume rendering of computed tomography (CT) may convey anatomy intuitively, but it needs the CT data, dedicated software and a powerful graphics processor. Three-dimensional Gaussian splatting (3DGS) displays photorealistic scenes in real time on less powerful devices, but existing medical approaches train each scene for minutes to hours. We aimed to convert a CT volume rendering directly into a 3DGS model without training. Splats were placed on an adaptive octree of millimetre-sized cells. Their colour and opacity were computed on the graphics processor with the renderer's lighting, cell opacity was converted analytically into splat opacity, and faint splats were retained stochastically above the 1/255 opacity floor of standard 3DGS rasterisers. Six public CT scans (1.5-mm voxels) were converted with three transfer functions and compared with converged references by peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR), structural similarity and a learned perceptual metric. Two-million-splat models reached a PSNR of 32.6 +/- 0.9 dB for bone, 30.1 +/- 1.0 dB for contrast-enhanced vessels and 32.3 +/- 1.5 dB for skin and bone. A conversion pass (usually two or three per model) took 4-30 s on a current laptop and 9-60 s on a desktop with a 4-GB graphics card. On the laptop, the models rendered at 68 frames per second at 800 x 800 pixels. Training-free conversion produced real-time 3DGS models of photorealistic CT renderings in about a minute or less per conversion pass on ordinary computers, with fidelity that we consider sufficient for interactive viewing.

Abstract:We present EmbeddingGemma 2, an open multimodal embedding model which natively represents text, images, audio, video, and combinations thereof in a unified space. Notably, in comprehensive embedding benchmarks across all modalities, EmbeddingGemma 2 establishes a new Pareto frontier, outperforming both specialist models and models two to twelve times larger. Furthermore, our design offers flexibility for resource-constrained environments, supporting selective modality encoder loading, adjustable vision token budgets, truncatable embedding dimensions, and quantized weights. We present ablations analyzing key decisions and release EmbeddingGemma 2 to promote further research.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Recently, it has been shown that vision-language models (VLMs) have a certain intrinsic understanding of time. They can estimate the temporal origin of objects and encode time along a low-dimensional, non-linear manifold inside their embedding space~\cite{tekaya2025matter}. That result is quantitative and reproducible, but the structure itself stays out of reach for end users: they cannot see it, query it with their own images, or inspect where a query lands relative to other years on the time axis. We present TimeLineXR, an interactive demonstration that turns this time axis into an explorable 3D scene. A web demo lets users upload or select images and see each placed on a color-coded 3D time axis; a this http URL WebXR scene renders the same time axis as a room-scale representation that can be grabbed, scaled, inspected, and queried by throwing image cards into it. Both clients run the same prediction pipeline against the same cached temporal index: encode the query with CLIP, project it through cosine-kernel KPCA, and snap it to the Bézier time axis. The system turns each image into a point on the time axis, so users can compare where different objects land and read off their estimated first year of this http URL source and reproduction steps can be found in: this https URL.

25. 【2610.37341】CodeCap-Bench: Can Code Serve as a Good Video Caption?

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37341

作者：Jiafu Tang,Yifan Xu,Eric Tang,Junjie Zhao,Shipeng Luo,Te Sun,Haoxuan Hu,Yiyan Ji,Xinlong Chen,Zihao Wang,Yang Shi,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Jiaheng Liu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：natural language, compact descriptions, Code, Video captioning aims, potentially ambiguous descriptions

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Video captioning aims to preserve visual information through compact descriptions, predominantly using natural language. However, precisely expressing fine-grained spatial structures and temporal dynamics in natural language often leads to lengthy and potentially ambiguous descriptions. In this work, we explore Code as Video Caption, a paradigm where executable programs serve as a complementary representation of visual content through graphical primitives, layout rules, and functions of time. To examine whether code captions effectively preserve video information, we compare them with natural language captions on downstream question answering and video reconstruction tasks. Our results show that code captions achieve superior reasoning accuracy and reconstruction fidelity. Despite these advantages, realizing the potential of code captioning depends on whether current multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can generate faithful code captions. To systematically assess this capability, we introduce CodeCap-Bench, which comprises 323 videos spanning five categories of structured 2D content, along with fine-grained annotations and a multi-level evaluation framework. Extensive experiments reveal that while leading proprietary MLLMs demonstrate promising code captioning capabilities, they still struggle to faithfully capture complex temporal dynamics, with open-source models lagging substantially behind.

26. 【2610.37326】ScIntBench: Physical Interaction Plausibility in 3D Scene Generation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37326

作者：Wendi Ying,Daochang Liu,Leonid Sigal,Ajmal Saeed Mian

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：reliability insufficiently assessed, generation largely emphasize, largely emphasize semantic, emphasize semantic fidelity, leaving interaction-time physical

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Existing evaluations of text-conditioned 3D indoor scene generation largely emphasize semantic fidelity and physical validity at rest, leaving interaction-time physical reliability insufficiently assessed. To address this gap, we introduce ScIntBench, a unified benchmark centered on interaction readiness: it executes prescribed manipulation actions (e.g., pushing, opening, and surface probing) in physics simulation and measures task success, capability and progress, post-interaction integrity, and dynamic plausibility. Multi-view semantic and static physical evaluations complement these metrics and help explain interaction outcomes. We systematically evaluate 12 state-of-the-art scene generation systems across 10 room types. Our results reveal that strong semantic and static physical performance does not necessarily translate into interaction readiness: a system with near-perfect static plausibility completes only 11.54% of interactions, 7 of the 12 systems produce scenes that cannot support any interaction, and even the best system succeeds in only 36.92% of prescribed tasks. These findings highlight two fundamental challenges in generating interaction-ready scenes: providing the necessary physical interaction capabilities and ensuring reliable physical behavior under active manipulation.

27. 【2610.37321】Hierarchical Sparse Graph for Multi-dimensional Assignment in Event-based Tracking

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37321

作者：Muxi Zha,Yang Shang,Wenlong Zhang,Qifeng Yu,Banglei Guan

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：methods typically operate, online manner, typically operate, tracking methods typically, adjacent batches

备注： 16 pages, published to IEEE T-CSVT

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Existing event-based tracking methods typically operate in an online manner, associating tracklets across adjacent batches. However, this limited temporal window often leads to failures in challenging scenarios, such as long-term occlusions or missed detections, where broader temporal context should be incorporated for high-precision tracking. To achieve robust and accurate tracking, this paper concentrates on the event-based multi-dimensional assignment that associates tracklets across multiple batches simultaneously. Unlike image-based detectors, event-based detectors can generate multiple correct yet spatially distinct tracklets for a single object per batch. This violates the one-to-one rule in classic data association, making event-based association an underdetermined many-to-many matching problem. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to tackle this NP-hard problem. To address its underdetermined nature, we formulate the assignment problem via fuzzy logic, where pairwise tracklet correlations are quantified through multiple long-range kinematic metrics. This fuzzy design eliminates the need for hard tracking thresholds and enables multiple heterogeneous metrics to be integrated into a unified correlation. By treating tracklets as nodes and their fuzzy correlations as edges, we construct a Hierarchical Sparse Graph (HSG). Building upon the HSG, we pose assignment as a graph partition task, and employ a heuristic solution that measures vertex affinity for partitioning adaptively. The entire pipeline is training-free. Comprehensive experiments on two challenging event datasets, eTram and Ev-UAV, show that our method outperforms existing event-based and most image-based association models. Particularly, it achieves over 2$\times$ the performance of OC-SORT across all metrics in the many-to-many association scenario.

Abstract:Skin cancer is among the most prevalent and life threatening forms of cancer worldwide. Early and accurate diagnosis significantly improves patient survival rates. 29. 【2610.37309】MvT: Event-based Multi-view Projection for Multiple Object Tracking

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37309
