类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Multiple Object Tracking, requires specialized methods, asynchronous event output, Multiple Object, cameras are increasingly

备注： 16 pages, 20 figures, published to IEEE T-IP

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Event cameras are increasingly used for Multiple Object Tracking (MOT), but their asynchronous event output often requires specialized methods. Existing processing methods primarily follow two paradigms, pseudo-frames and event-by-event. The former is the prevailing approach since its data format aligns with images, making image-based techniques applicable. However, it suffers from tracking failures when trajectories overlap or are spatially close on pseudo-frames. Facing this challenge, we propose a multi-view pipeline, Multi-view Tracking (MvT), which preserves the 2D data format to leverage image-based techniques directly while introducing additional spatio-temporal views to resolve tracking ambiguities in a single view. MvT comprises a Multi-view Projection (MvP) module and a Multi-view Fusion (MvF) stage. MvP encodes events into three complementary spatio-temporal views while mitigating the pattern discretization. Within MvF, multi-view results are unified into a 3D coordinate system, and tracklets are associated through an optimization model subject to specific criteria combination. Evaluations on four datasets, including our self-collected Small Objects Dataset (SOD), show that MvT seamlessly integrates image-based methods and outperforms existing non-learning and learning trackers in generalized scenarios, and effectively resolves the single-view tracking ambiguities. Being training-free, MvT is applicable when ground-truth annotation is infeasible, thereby highlighting its practical, data-efficient potential. Code is available at this https URL.

30. 【2610.37297】RUCO: A Scalable Lock-Free Algorithm for Parallel Contour Extraction

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37297

作者：Rafael Munoz-Salinas,Francisco J.Romero-Ramirez,Manuel J. Marin-Jimenez

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Transforming binary masks, Transforming binary, high-resolution segmentation outputs, post-processing high-resolution segmentation, segmentation outputs

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Transforming binary masks into geometric contours remains a critical step in many computer vision pipelines, particularly for post-processing high-resolution segmentation outputs. Yet, standard algorithms such as the Suzuki-Abe method are limited by their sequential nature and the overhead of building topological hierarchies. As modern applications transition to high-resolution imaging, there is a critical need for contour extraction that leverages multi-core CPU architectures without the complexity of tile stitching or heavy synchronization. This paper presents TRUCO (Threaded Raster Unrestricted Contour Ownership), a lock-free parallel algorithm designed for high-performance geometric boundary extraction. TRUCO introduces a start-point ownership rule, combined with a speculative tracing model, in which threads trace contours downward into adjacent regions and use an upward-termination logic to ensure each contour is processed exactly once. Experimental results on synthetic and real-world datasets, conducted on both consumer and server-grade CPUs, demonstrate that TRUCO significantly outperforms existing baselines. The method achieves speedups of over an order of magnitude compared to the industry standard while maintaining robust scalability on high-resolution scenes across diverse hardware architectures.

31. 【2610.37290】aming Noise Initialization for Zero-shot Video Restoration with Image Diffusion Priors

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37290

作者：Chong Wang,Zixuan Fu,Hao Cheng,Siyuan Yang,Alex C. Kot,Bihan Wen

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Recent advances, achieved remarkable success, restoring high-quality images, achieved remarkable, restoring high-quality

备注： IEEE Transactions on Multimedia

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Recent advances in diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in restoring high-quality images in a zero-shot manner. Unfortunately, extending this success to video restoration is challenging due to the substantial computational resources required to pre-train video diffusion models. A rising trend seeks to address video restoration by utilizing a pre-trained image diffusion model, initiating restoration from either frame-wise independent or identical Gaussian noise. Yet, these approaches often fall short, as such simple noise initiations fail to capture and utilize the inherent temporal correlations within degraded video frames. In this work, we propose taming noise initialization for zero-shot video restoration, which preserves the intrinsic temporal correlations by generating initial noises directly using DDIM inversion on the corrupted video frames themselves. Building on this, we introduce an adaptive noise blending strategy that suppresses degradation patterns encoded during DDIM inversion while gradually steering the noise toward a Gaussian distribution. To further emphasize temporal consistency, we propose measurement-guided trajectory attention to better align features across frames. Extensive experiments on multiple video restoration tasks demonstrate that our method outperforms existing zero-shot approaches, consistently achieving superior results on the DAVIS, REDS4, and Set8 datasets.

32. 【2610.37267】GradCO: Behavior-Aware Gradient-Conflict Offloading for Text-Based Person Anomaly Search

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37267

作者：Zhiheng Fu,Zixu Li,Zhiwei Chen,Jiale Huang,Fangxu Liu,Jinhe Bi,Yupeng Hu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：Person Anomaly Search, Text-based Person Anomaly, Anomaly Search, Person Anomaly, Text-based Person

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Text-based Person Anomaly Search (TPAS) retrieves a target pedestrian using natural-language descriptions of appearance and normal or anomalous behavior. Unlike conventional text-based person retrieval, TPAS must preserve identity cues while distinguishing fine-grained behaviors. It presents two challenges: same-identity, different-behavior hard negatives cause gradient conflict during granular alignment, while strong appearance cues can obscure weak behavioral evidence during aggregation. We propose GradCO (behavior-aware Gradient-Conflict Offloading), comprising three complementary components. Behavior-Aware Mixture-of-Experts (B-MoE) forms appearance, normal-behavior, and anomalous-behavior streams whose roles are learned from retrieval objectives without expert-specific semantic labels. Conflict Offload Learning (COL) uses pair-conditioned offload anchors in similarity computation to reduce the conflicting-gradient burden on the main text representations. Hierarchical Intent Alignment (HIA) aggregates expert similarities and features using query-conditioned weights. Experiments on five TPAS/TIPR benchmarks show GradCO significantly surpasses SOTA methods and validate our design.

33. 【2610.37257】GameVerse: A Minute-Scale Gameplay Dataset for Long-Horizon Interactive World Modeling

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37257

备注： Accepted by NeurIPS 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Recent advances in interactive world models have made it possible to generate controllable, dynamic environments from action inputs. Yet current models struggle to maintain coherence beyond roughly one minute of rollout, a limitation rooted not in model capacity but in the scarcity of long-horizon training data. Existing gameplay video datasets typically consist of clips under ten seconds, with captions that describe whole clips in coarse and undifferentiated text, which is insufficient for controllable long-horizon generation. We address this with GameVerse, a minute-scale gameplay video dataset purpose-built for long-horizon interactive world modeling. GameVerse contains 69.7K clips spanning 1,655 hours and 349.6 million frames across diverse first- and third-person gameplay, including 1,358 hours of continuous two-minute sequences, an order of magnitude longer than prior datasets. Clips pass through a unified pipeline of scene-consistent segmentation, quality filtering, camera pose estimation, and event-based caption annotation. Each clip is paired with a temporally stable camera trajectory that remains coherent across minutes, together with captions that separate static scene context from dynamic foreground events and align to event boundaries rather than fixed time windows. Comprehensive analysis shows that GameVerse surpasses existing public datasets in scale, diversity, and annotation quality. Experiments further demonstrate that training on GameVerse substantially extends the temporal horizon of interactive world models.

34. 【2610.37236】HDVS: semi-supervised semantic segmentation via heterogeneous dual-branch voting supervision

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37236

作者：Yongqi Shan,Yunzhi Zhuge,Huchuan Lu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Semi-supervised semantic segmentation, achieve accurate segmentation, accurate segmentation results, fewer human annotations, attracted increasing attention

备注： 16 pages, 9 figures, 7 tables. Published in Visual Intelligence

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Semi-supervised semantic segmentation, which aims to utilize large volumes of unlabeled images to achieve accurate segmentation results with fewer human annotations, has attracted increasing attention. Prior methods were primarily based on the assumption that the pseudo-labels generated by each branch were sufficiently reliable to supervise the remaining branches. However, there is a concern that the errors in pseudo-labels could accumulate during co-training, potentially resulting in suboptimal performance. To this end, we present a novel framework called heterogeneous dual-branch voting supervision (HDVS), which is designed to enhance the reliability of pseudo-labels and mitigate the issues arising from pseudo-labeling. Specifically, based on the cross-supervision framework, we introduce a voting mechanism that correlates pseudo-labels from heterogeneous branches to produce pseudo-labels with enhanced reliability. Concurrently, we employ a feature communication module to introduce perturbations at the feature level in each branch to maximize prediction diversity across the dual branches while maintaining convergence. Comprehensive evaluations of the proposed HDVS on two benchmark datasets (PASCAL VOC 2012 and Cityscapes) demonstrate its superiority to the state-of-the-art approaches.

35. 【2610.37226】Subspace Clifford Networks

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37226

作者：Zhongping Ji

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：replaces global cyclic, global cyclic rolling, propose Subspace Clifford, Subspace Clifford Networks, reformulation that replaces

备注： 12 pages

点击查看摘要

Abstract:In this work, we propose Subspace Clifford Networks, a reformulation that replaces global cyclic rolling with grouped subspace interactions. Instead of sampling sparse cyclic diagonals across the entire $D$-dimensional channel space, we project the features into an aligned space $\R^{\tilde{D}} = \bigoplus_{g=1}^{G} V_g$ partitioned into $G$ low-dimensional groups $V_g \cong \R^k$ with $k \in \{2, 3, 4\}$. Within each $k$-dimensional group, we compute all $\binom{k}{2}$ wedge coordinates and concatenate them with $\operatorname{SiLU}$-activated coordinate-wise products, while sandwiching the grouped interaction between learnable $1\times 1$ linear projections to enable cross-group channel mixing. This design reduces intermediate feature expansion from $2|\mathcal{S}|D$ (typically $10D$ for 5 shifts) down to $1.5\tilde{D}$ ($k=2$), $2.0\tilde{D}$ ($k=3$), or $2.5\tilde{D}$ ($k=4$). Furthermore, exploiting the small, fixed group size $k \in \{2, 3, 4\}$, we implement a fused CUDA kernel that evaluates the forward interaction and a recomputation-based analytical backward pass using thread-local variables without allocating global-memory buffers for elementwise intermediates. Finally, we extend the backbone from an isotropic columnar layout to a multi-resolution Hierarchical Clifford Network with configurable per-stage subspace dimensions. Experiments on CIFAR-100 show that the proposed models substantially reduce parameter count and training time across both regimes, while the hierarchical variants also improve classification accuracy over the evaluated shift-based hierarchical baselines.

36. 【2610.37219】Vision-Language-Action or World-Action? Just Action Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37219

作者：Yonghao Yu,Lang Huang,Runyi Li,Toshihiko Yamasaki

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Robotics (cs.RO)

关键词：inherit pretrained knowledge, communities debate, world-action model, JAM, visual generators

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:The vision-language-action (VLA) and world-action model (WAM) communities debate whether control policies should inherit pretrained knowledge from vision-language models or from visual generators. The debate, however, bundles three separable choices: backbone initialization, the objective applied to future observations, and whether the machinery for that future loss is kept at inference. A gap between a VLA and a WAM therefore cannot simply be attributed to any one of them. We show that decoupling these choices yields a common framework for both families, which we term Just Action Model (JAM): a pretrained visual backbone with an action expert, trained with an action loss and a foresight loss. The foresight loss trains current-observation features to predict frozen encodings of the recorded future through a prediction head discarded after training, so no visual generation is involved in either training or inference, and the backbone can be chosen freely. We first compare objectives within fixed policy architectures, then evaluate the foresight recipe across backbone families. The resulting JAM policies outperform their matched FastWAM and ImageWAM counterparts on LIBERO and LIBERO-Plus, and achieve similar or slightly higher success rates on RoboTwin. Compared with FastWAM, JAM improves the success rates from 97.0%/55.3%/91.2% to 98.2%/64.5%/91.4%, respectively. With cached foresight targets, JAM reduces estimated online training FLOPs per sample per update by 73% relative to FastWAM. Our results show that future supervision improves control without visual generation or additional inference machinery, and strong foresight performance is attainable with either generator-initialized or vision-language backbones. 37. 【2610.37218】opoField: Technical Report for ECCV 2026 SceneFun3D Challenge

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37218

作者：Zhiwei Chen,Zhiheng Fu,Zixu Li,Mingyu Zhang,Jiale Huang,Yupeng Hu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Functional segmentation requires, small interactive parts, segmentation requires locating, requires locating small, locating small interactive

备注： Technical Report for ECCV OpenSUN3D Workshop SceneFun3D Benchmark Challenge

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Functional segmentation requires locating small interactive parts, such as knobs, buttons, and sockets, in indoor point clouds and returning confidence-ranked instance masks. A point-wise semantic predictor produces a continuous probability field, whereas the benchmark evaluates a discrete set of scored instances. This creates a substantial field-to-instance gap. We present TopoField, a deterministic decoder built on top of VOLT. TopoField first converts 13 test-time forward passes into a semantic consensus field, then decodes instance candidates from the consensus geometry, and finally ranks the candidates in a class-aware manner. The augmentation stage retains the processed-point identity of every fragment prediction, accumulates probabilities in a common point array, normalizes by coverage count, and fuses the resulting caches. The decoding stage separates high-confidence cores from broader support regions, constructs a symmetric radius-pruned point graph, and uses a multi-source graph-geodesic partition to split support regions that contain multiple seeds. On 561 ground-truth instances across 30 validation scenes, an IoU-0.5-valid candidate exists for 67.2% of instances, and top-1 coverage is 20.0%. These complementary measurements motivate joint attention to candidate construction and ranking. TopoField achieves 28.286% AP, 41.383% AP50, and 47.558% AP25. TopoField achieved second place on the ECCV 2026 SceneFun3D Challenge.

Abstract:This report presents the implementation and validation of two end-to-end multimodal adaptation pipelines for 3D-aware visual question answering on a custom spatial dataset. The first pipeline fine-tunes HiSpatial-3B, which augments RGB observations with metric-scale 3D point maps; the second adapts Qwen3-VL through memory-efficient QLoRA-based supervised fine-tuning. The completed engineering stack covers dataset conversion, image-annotation alignment, monocular geometry preprocessing, instruction construction, mixed-domain training, checkpoint management, batch inference, and automated evaluation. On the target spatial benchmark, HiSpatial adaptation increases overall accuracy from 1.73% before fine-tuning to 79.89% at 3,600 steps. On a separately collected styled split, accuracy improves from 14.9% to 55.1%, indicating transfer beyond the original training set. 39. 【2610.37211】Uncovering Shortcut Amplification in Video RLVR through the Lens of Perceptual Uncertainty Collapse

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37211

作者：Jiafeng Liang,Xianhao Ou,Tao Ren,Rongchuan Mu,Runxuan Liu,Dandan Tu,Ming Liu,See-Kiong Ng,Bing Qin

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：Large Multimodal Models, Large Multimodal, effective post-training paradigm, Reinforcement learning, capability of Large

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as an effective post-training paradigm for enhancing the video reasoning capability of Large Multimodal Models (LMMs). Despite its empirical effectiveness, we show that outcome-level optimization can introduce an undesirable shortcut amplification effect, whereby models increasingly favor text-prior answers even when they are not supported by the visual evidence. To characterize this behavior, we investigate the training dynamics of video RLVR through the lens of perceptual uncertainty, analyzing how predictive uncertainty evolves under video-conditioned and text-only contexts. Our analysis identifies a perceptual uncertainty collapse phenomenon, in which RL training continuously sharpens the model's answer distribution while the uncertainty reduction attributable to visual evidence progressively diminishes. A further gradient-direction analysis reveals that such confidence enhancement becomes increasingly less aligned with visually grounded uncertainty reduction and is instead dominated by text-prior compression. Motivated by these findings, we propose Bifocal Self-distillation Policy Optimization (BSPO), a self-distillation framework that regulates policy updates with two complementary distributional signals. Specifically, BSPO derives empirical reasoning principles from intra-group correct and incorrect rollouts to identify reasoning-sensitive tokens, and introduces shortcut-aware visual perturbation to penalize predictions that remain invariant when task-relevant visual evidence is weakened. By adaptively reweighting the original RL advantage, BSPO preserves the task-solving benefits of RLVR while promoting updates that are both reasoning-sensitive and visually grounded. Extensive experiments validate the effectiveness of BSPO in improving video reasoning performance and strengthening visual faithfulness.

Abstract:Current 3D scene understanding pipelines typically concatenate vast numbers of 3D object tokens with text tokens and feed the resulting sequence to a Large Language Model (LLM). Evaluations show that 3D-SceneQ outperforms current state-of-the-art methods across multiple benchmarks (e.g., +5.7 CIDEr on Scan2Cap and +17.0 EM@1 on SQA3D), demonstrating its remarkable capabilities in various 3D language reasoning tasks.

41. 【2610.37196】How Much Can Best-of-N Help a World Action Model? Measuring Selectable Headroom and Realizing It in Closed Loop

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37196

作者：Haojia Gao,Yue Cao,Yumin Liu,Haohua Que,Mingkai Liu,Guanqiu Guo,Zhuo Zou,Bokui Chen,Xueqian Wang

类目：Robotics (cs.RO); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：World action models, makes test-time selection, World action, visual consequences, makes test-time

备注： 24 pages, 12 figures, 14 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:World action models (WAMs) predict the visual consequences of the actions they generate, which makes test-time selection a natural way to improve a fixed policy: imagine the futures of candidate actions and execute the best one. The benefit of selection is bounded by the selectable headroom, how much better the best candidate truly is, but the standard estimate, which executes each candidate once and keeps the best outcome, conflates candidate quality with the randomness of everything that follows. We introduce a denoised counterfactual protocol, based on bitwise-reproducible branching, repeated continuations, held-out oracles, and common random numbers, that measures the headroom a selector can realize, and use its measurements to build a fail-gated selector. Across four WAMs, two benchmarks, over 100,000 branched rollouts, and pre-registered tests, we find that (i) the standard estimate overstates the selectable headroom of native samples: bounds from 17 continuations per candidate place its selectable share between 24% and 66% for three WAMs; (ii) structured action perturbations create up to 16 points of selectable headroom; and (iii) in both WAMs with action-conditioned imagination, imagined futures carry candidate-outcome information with no detectable loss relative to real futures, information that the value head of Cosmos Policy leaves unused. Deviating from the policy chunk only when a readout of the WAM predicts failure, the selector raises closed-loop task success from 76.9% to 81.6% on held-out episodes of training tasks, with the largest gain where the policy fails most often, and from 62.6% to 69.8% on unseen tasks.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Open-vocabulary 3D occupancy prediction represents scene geometry and semantics in a unified voxel grid, enabling recognition beyond predefined categories. One approach reconstructs scenes with 3D Gaussians and converts them into voxel occupancy. However, Gaussian scale inflation and excessive overlap can distort voxel occupancy predictions, while semantic mixing can arise within Gaussians spanning multiple categories and during 3D--2D semantic feature alignment. We propose GCSR-OCC, which combines geometry calibration and multi-mode query-conditioned semantic routing for indoor occupancy prediction from a single RGB image. Abstract:Weakly-supervised instance segmentation (WSIS) based on either box-, point-, or image-level annotations has been proposed to overcome the labor-intensive annotation process in fully-supervised instance segmentation. Therefore, we propose the Mixed-Supervised Instance Segmentation (MixSIS) task, which allows a flexible mix of annotation levels (image, point, box, and mask) within a dataset. We evaluate AnnsMixer on both WSIS and MixSIS tasks, largely outperforming state-of-the-art methods. In three case studies, we also demonstrate how the unique features of MixSIS and AnnsMixer can substantially reduce the annotation time (-90%) or improve the results (+34%) in instance segmentation with a simple annotation rule or leveraging heterogeneous data. Our code is available at this https URL

47. 【2610.37135】Physics Guided Diffusion Model Based Exposure Interpolation for High Dynamic Range Imaging

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37135

作者：Zhengguo Li,Chaobing Zheng

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：popular multi-shot based, Ghosting artifacts, dynamic range, standard dynamic range, exposure interpolation algorithm

备注： 11 pages, 6 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Ghosting artifacts are an open problem for popular multi-shot based high dynamic range (HDR) imaging. An effective solution is to capture two large-exposure-ratio (LER) standard dynamic range (SDR) images simultaneously via a single-shot. Brightness order reversal (BOR) artifacts are generated when the two LER images are directly fused together. It is crucial to interpolate an image with an exposure time in between the exposure times of two LER images. In this paper, a novel physics-guided diffusion model based exposure interpolation algorithm is proposed to address the BOR artifacts. An initial image is interpolated by a physics-driven component, and the residual high-frequency details are generated by a new diffusion model based component. The new diffusion model is based on the correlation analysis of generative models and includes a new predicted target to reduce the variation of the Pearson correlation between the noisy data and the target, as well as the amplification factor of the fitting error. Compared with existing neural augmented exposure interpolation algorithms on top of convolution neural networks, the proposed exposure interpolation algorithm has an inherent advantage to generate more high frequency information. Experiment results validate the proposed exposure interpolation algorithm.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37134

Abstract:Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) is routinely used to diagnose spinal disorders, but surgical planning often requires Computed Tomography (CT) for better visualization of vertebral bone anatomy. Synthesizing CT from MRI could avoid additional radiation exposure and streamline preoperative assessment. We propose a three-stage framework for spine MRI-to-CT synthesis. First, an anatomy-guided vertebral matching system identifies similar sagittal MRI and CT slices using spine segmentations and aligns them through rigid registration, creating pseudo-paired data from unpaired scans. Second, these pairs train an adversarial diffusion model for MRI-to-CT translation. Third, synthesized slices are assembled into 3D volumes and refined by a network that improves inter-slice continuity and structural coherence. The framework was trained on over 10,000 unpaired sagittal MRI and CT slices and evaluated on 1,911 slices from 83 MRI volumes spanning all spinal regions. Assessment combined distribution-based measures with vertebral segmentation metrics. Compared with diffusion alone, the proposed approach increased binary 3D vertebral Dice from 0.666 to 0.739. Volumetric post-processing reduced visible discontinuities between stacked slices and improved vertebral coherence in axial and coronal views. Combining anatomy-guided pseudo-pairing, adversarial diffusion and 3D refinement improves spine MRI-to-CT synthesis. Vertebral matching primarily enhanced anatomical alignment and fidelity, while volumetric refinement improved 3D consistency. These findings support further development of MRI-derived CT-like visualization for preoperative planning, particularly to preserve fine intensity details while maintaining volumetric coherence.

51. 【2610.37110】A First-Step Bias Restores Lost Diversity in Diffusion Model Distillation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37110

作者：Wenwu Tang,Dong Wang,Yun Cheng,Olga Saukh

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：Distribution-matching distillation enables, distillation enables high-quality, Distribution-matching distillation, enables high-quality image, high-quality image generation

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Distribution-matching distillation enables high-quality image generation in only a few denoising steps. However, distilled student models often map different initial noise samples to similar outputs under the same prompt. To locate where diversity is lost, we compare the teacher and student along the sampling trajectory. The largest measured reduction in the response to different initial noises occurs during the first, high-noise step. When we replace the first step with the teacher's corresponding trajectory and leave the final three student steps unchanged, most of the output diversity returns. This suggests that the first step is a key point for restoring diversity. Motivated by this finding, we introduce \emph{ReSeed}, a post-distillation method that adds a seed-response bias adapter only to the first student step and aligns its velocity with the teacher's first-interval secant velocity. All student weights and later denoising steps remain unchanged. We evaluate ReSeed on three text-to-image models (SD3-Medium, SD3.5-Medium, and FLUX.1-dev) and one text-to-video model (Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B). For a distilled 4-step FLUX.1-dev model, \emph{ReSeed} requires only $0.00196\%$ additional trainable parameters relative to the base model and is trained on 8,000 prompt--noise pairs, while keeping the distilled student fully frozen and requiring no target images, perceptual losses, or adversarial losses. It improves DINO diversity by $48.3\%$ while maintaining competitive visual quality. These results show that substantial diversity can be recovered through a lightweight, data-efficient tuning of only the first denoising step, without finetuning the full student model.

53. 【2610.37097】riDifNet: A Unified Three-Task Diffusion Network for End-to-End RGB-T Perception

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37097

作者：Qianchao He,Jing Liu,Yunhao Li,Xiaoqing Tian

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：supports robust scene, robust scene understanding, multi-modal perception supports, perception supports robust, Diffusion Module

备注： 16 pages, 6 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Infrared--visible (IR--VIS) multi-modal perception supports robust scene understanding under challenging illumination. Infrared and visible images are often misaligned due to platform motion, viewpoint changes, and modality-dependent distortions, causing registration errors to propagate into subsequent fusion and detection stages. We propose TriDifNet, a unified three-task diffusion network for end-to-end RGB-T perception. To the best of our knowledge, TriDifNet is the first diffusion-based network that jointly optimizes registration, fusion, and detection for misaligned RGB-T pedestrian perception. TriDifNet consists of three diffusion-based modules, i.e., the Registration Diffusion Module (RegDM), the Fusion Diffusion Module (FusDM), and the Detection Diffusion Module (DetDM). RegDM aligns the unaligned infrared image to the optical reference by estimating a dense deformation field. FusDM performs residual conditional diffusion to generate a detection-oriented fused representation with optical structural cues and complementary infrared saliency. DetDM formulates pedestrian detection as an iterative denoising process in bounding-box space. To couple the three tasks, TriDifNet introduces a closed-loop optimization mechanism, where detection semantics guide fusion and registration, and the fusion result further regularizes geometric alignment. A stage-wise progressive training strategy and a lightweight Nash multi-task learning (Nash-MTL)-based late-stage refinement are further adopted to balance registration, fusion, and detection optimization. Extensive experiments on the KAIST multispectral pedestrian benchmark show that TriDifNet achieves superior downstream detection performance under misaligned RGB-T settings. Against strong system-level competitors, TriDifNet achieves 65.44\% Recall, 57.55\% mAP$_{50}$, and 26.83\% mAP$_{50:95}$.

备注： Project page: [this https URL](https://haoyu-agent.github.io/SceneEvolve)

Abstract:Reconstructing editable and compositional 3D scenes from a real image requires jointly reasoning about geometry, object structure, and spatial relationships. We introduce SceneEvolve, a framework that represents 3D scenes as executable code and improves reconstruction by evolving the harness around a small open-source agent. SceneEvolve combines SceneAct, a compositional semantic action interface built on parameterized primitives, hierarchical composition, and semantic spatial relations, with SceneHarness-RSI, which leverages a stronger teacher agent to iteratively evolve the harness using feedback from development-set reconstructions. Throughout harness evolution, the model parameters, action semantics, and execution backend remain fixed. The resulting harness provides a reusable reconstruction procedure that enables the small open-source model to reconstruct unseen scenes without parameter updates or teacher assistance. Across two in-domain datasets and one out-of-domain dataset, SceneEvolve with the comparatively compact Qwen3.8-27B model improves all four evaluated reconstruction metrics while reducing mean inference time by 47.0% relative to the base model. More notably, despite relying on a substantially smaller model, SceneEvolve achieves 32.3% higher reconstruction quality than the large proprietary GPT-5.6-Sol model, while reducing mean equivalent deployment cost by 59.8%. Furthermore, the harness evolved using Qwen3.8-27B also generalizes to Kimi-K3, achieving comparable reconstruction performance to GPT-6-Astra. The project page is this https URL.

57. 【2610.37037】Zooming without Zooming v2: From Seeing Details to Connecting Evidence for Generalizable Multimodal Perception

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37037

作者：Lai Wei,Yatai Li,Liangbo He,Jun Lan,Renjie Gu,Linhan Cao,Junkai Lin,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Linghe Kong,Yue Wang,Zhuosheng Zhang,Weiran Huang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：forms the foundation, Multimodal, Multimodal perception forms, multimodal intelligence, tasks

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Multimodal perception forms the foundation of multimodal intelligence. In particular, spatial perception and multimodal long-context understanding are two pivotal tasks that underpin embodied AI and multimodal agents. However, synthesizing targeted, high-quality training data for these tasks remains difficult and costly. Our key insight is that these tasks share an underlying capability, which we call connecting evidence: identifying relevant visual facts, binding them to the correct entities, recovering their relations, and composing them into an answer. Crucially, targeted training data are far easier to synthesize for this capability than for these tasks themselves. To strengthen this capability, we propose EvidenceConnect, an easy-to-implement data synthesis framework that uses zoomed views of related regions within a single image to generate questions requiring comparison, aggregation, or chaining of visual evidence. To ensure quality, a reference answer is retained only when independent VLMs agree on it, and region-ablation tests discard questions whose answer survives the removal of selected regions. We then train base VLMs on the resulting data with RLVR, obtaining Zooming without Zooming v2 (ZwZ2). Although the synthesized data are not tailored to any downstream benchmark, ZwZ2 improves fine-grained and spatial perception, with further gains on embodied spatial reasoning, multimodal long-context understanding, multimodal agent tasks, and general visual reasoning. These results establish EvidenceConnect as an effective framework for supervising evidence connection and suggest that strengthening this shared capability leads to more generalizable multimodal perception.

58. 【2610.37036】GraftRay: Physically Based Mesh Insertion into Gaussian Splatting Scenes via Path Tracing

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37036

作者：Jinxin Ai,Matthias Niessner

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Graphics (cs.GR)

关键词：Inserting virtual objects, synthetic data generation, Inserting virtual, Gaussian Splatting, content creation

备注： 8 pages + references, 6 figures, 2 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Inserting virtual objects into scenes reconstructed via 2D or 3D Gaussian Splatting (2DGS/3DGS) is a key capability for content creation, AR, and synthetic data generation. However, realistic insertion requires the object to be seamlessly lit by the scene and, conversely, to cast physically plausible shadows onto it. Existing approaches either harmonize appearance in image space, sacrificing multi-view consistency, or approximate scene illumination with an environment map at the insertion point, which cannot represent near-field area lights such as windows and lamps that dominate indoor lighting. To address this, we present GraftRay, a unified method for physically consistent insertion of mesh objects into indoor scenes represented by either 2D or 3D Gaussians. We recover scene materials and surface normals with diffusion-based priors, and explicitly model spatially-extended emitters, solving their radiance in closed form. A hybrid renderer then jointly path-traces the scene Gaussians and inserted meshes, producing view-consistent shading, interreflections, and soft shadows. On FIPT, GraftRay sets a new state of the art, outperforming recent baselines such as ComGS and EAG-PT in rendering fidelity and shadow accuracy, while maintaining highly competitive rendering efficiency.

Abstract:Continuous and up-to-date crop type mapping is essential for agricultural monitoring but is often hampered by limited reference data. 61. 【2610.37019】V-LUMEN: Visual Lookup Memory for Embedding Scaling in Vision-Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37019

作者：Miso Choi,Daekeun Kim,Eunji Kim,Jungbeom Lee

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Embedding-level scaling expands, increasing model depth, Embedding-level scaling, dense computation, scaling expands

备注： Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Embedding-level scaling expands the capacity of language models through lookup-based memory rather than by increasing model depth, width, or dense computation. Extending this idea to vision-language models (VLMs) is challenging because visual features are continuous, spatially structured, and query-dependent, unlike discrete text tokens that naturally support n-gram lookup. We introduce V-LUMEN, a visual lookup memory for VLMs. V-LUMEN discretizes visual features with a frozen codebook, builds spatially aware memory keys through spatial aggregation-based hashing, and modulates them with text-conditioned relevance signals; all addressing is non-parametric, and the retrieved embeddings are fused into the backbone through a gated residual pathway. On five reasoning-intensive benchmarks, V-LUMEN substantially improves a frozen Qwen3-VL-2B backbone and transfers zero-shot to three benchmarks whose images are disjoint from the training data. At a matched parameter budget, moving part of a LoRA or MoE adapter's capacity into V-LUMEN preserves accuracy on two backbones, indicating that external visual memory is an independent capacity axis rather than a stronger adaptation method. Because lookup cost is constant in table size and incurred only once at prefill, this capacity grows in storage rather than in per-token compute. Code is available at this https URL.

62. 【2610.37015】RePAIR: Inverting Backdoor Attacks in Semantic Segmentation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37015

作者：Arnold Brosch,Kira Maag

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Cryptography and Security (cs.CR); Probability (math.PR)

关键词：computer vision, posing a security, security risk, significant advances, advances in computer

备注： 26 pages total, 9 pages main paper, 2 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Despite significant advances in computer vision, semantic segmentation models remain vulnerable to backdoor attacks, posing a security risk. These attacks redirect pixels of a chosen victim class to an attacker-specified target class when a trigger is present, while preserving clean predictions. Existing defenses are largely derived from image classification, while dedicated segmentation defenses remain scarce and typically require retraining or model modification. We introduce RePAIR, a post-hoc, label-free, attack-unaware defense based on the observation that backdoor-induced shifts can be stronger in the classifier head than in internal representations, leaving recoverable victim-class semantics. RePAIR infers the victim-target relation from an unlabeled, potentially contaminated image pool, constructs a safety-approved semantic reference, and localizes suspicious pixels by comparing head predictions with internal semantic evidence. It then applies feature-space and output corrections toward the inferred victim semantics, while conservative localization and output constraints limit changes to benign predictions. We model the attack as adversarial probability transport and repair as its approximate reversal, deriving bounds and sufficient conditions for localization, residual attack success, clean-side changes, defense-induced target predictions, and attack-unaware pair identification. Experiments across segmentation architectures and attack settings show that RePAIR substantially reduces attack success while largely preserving clean performance.

63. 【2610.37000】GPA-VGGT: Geometry-Preserving Acceleration of VGGT

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37000

作者：Muyeol Sung,Jangwon Kim,Jongwoo Lim

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：recover camera parameters, single forward pass, Feed-forward geometry models, VGGT recover camera, depth maps

备注： Accepted to WACV 2027. Project page: [this https URL](https://noheat61.github.io/GPA-VGGT/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Feed-forward geometry models such as VGGT recover camera parameters, depth maps, and point maps from one to hundreds of views in a single forward pass, making them powerful backbones for multi-view 3D perception. However, VGGT's global attention is quadratic in the number of frames and dominates end-to-end runtime on long sequences, which has motivated a range of training-free efficiency methods such as token merging. These methods, however, come with a drop in accuracy that makes them hard to use in place of VGGT. We propose GPA-VGGT, a training-free acceleration method that replaces global attention in early blocks with frame attention augmented by a shared cross-frame K/V set. In the remaining blocks, we merge only keys and values while preserving all queries, restore the attention mass of the tokens each merged key represents, and randomly project the matching metric to 128 dimensions to reduce matching cost. Across diverse multi-view geometry tasks, GPA-VGGT provides training-free acceleration at speedups comparable to prior work while preserving the information required for precise geometric estimation, retaining over 99% of VGGT's pose accuracy under AUC@3.

64. 【2610.36986】LearnPrunerV2: Learnable Progressive Token Pruning for Vision-Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36986

作者：Rinyoichi Takezoe,Yaqian Li,Weiren Wang,Zihao Bo,Huihong Shi,Anzhou Hou,Mo Guang,Jiale Yan,Kaiwen Long

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：demonstrated remarkable multimodal, remarkable multimodal capabilities, prohibitive computational overhead, computational overhead due, Vision-Language Models

备注： Accepted to ACM Multimedia 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated remarkable multimodal capabilities but suffer from prohibitive computational overhead due to the massive number of visual tokens. While token pruning is a promising solution, existing methods still suffer from two major drawbacks: 1) unreliable pruning criteria that fail to accurately localize task-specific regions due to inherent attention biases or query-agnostic designs, and 2) suboptimal pruning strategies that employ rigid, non-adaptive schedules, overlooking the dynamic evolution of token redundancy across different model depths. To address these challenges, we propose LearnPrunerV2, which extends the previous two-stage heuristic framework (LearnPruner) into a fully learnable architecture. Specifically, we integrate lightweight learnable modules into every layer of the VLM backbone, which estimate token importance by fusing intrinsic visual saliency with extrinsic query relevance captured via a shadow token. By uncovering a consistent staircase redundancy pattern across VLM layers, we further introduce Budget-Deterministic Pruning (BDP) to establish a hardware-friendly schedule with fixed per-layer budgets. Extensive experimental results across various benchmarks and VLMs demonstrate that LearnPrunerV2 consistently outperform previous state-of-the-art methods. Notably, LearnPrunerV2 preserves 99.8% of the original performance when pruning 77.8% of visual tokens on LLaVA-v1.5, achieving a superior accuracy-efficiency trade-off.

65. 【2610.36978】Explicit Regional Representations For Video Quality Assessment

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36978

作者：Fuyi Yang,Lei Yang,Andrew Liu,Chenyu Wei,Xiaoyi Guo,Ming Kai Hsu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

备注： Preprint. This work is an initial version and may be updated in future revisions

点击查看摘要

Abstract:End-to-end video quality assessment (VQA) methods leave the regional evidence underlying their predictions largely implicit. We introduce an explicit pixel-level regional representation for VQA, instantiated as a Quality-Relevance Heatmap (QRH) that combines localized degradation evidence with perceptual priors under vision-language model guidance, without requiring pixel-level annotations. We apply QRH to existing VQA models through two deterministic fragment-sampling strategies: distribution-preserving sampling approximates the original sampler's QRH-score distribution without retraining, while quality-aware sampling prioritizes regions with high QRH scores and is paired with fine-tuning. Experiments with DOVER on multiple benchmarks show that the former closely approximates predictions averaged over random samples, while the latter improves most evaluated metrics across popular VQA benchmarks. These results support explicit regional representations as a useful interface for downstream VQA.

Antiquarian scholarship thus cannot replace modern transcription, but it can steer, check, and segment HTR across the untranscribed registers.

Abstract:Mobile GUI agents are typically fine-tuned 7B-scale vision-language models (VLMs) that generate each action as text. 点击查看摘要

Abstract:GUI grounding on high-resolution screens fails mostly on small targets, and zooming in is a common training-free remedy. However, most existing zooming pipelines do not match the zoom to the target's size, accept the zoomed answer unconditionally and never revisit a wrong answer. To address these issues, we propose AdaGround, a training-free framework with three components: (1) Token-Normalized Adaptive Zooming, which sets the magnification, field of view and zoom depth of each sample from the model's own estimate of the target size; (2) Verifier-in-the-Loop Refinement, which uses one marked-click Yes/No verifier to keep or reject each zoom, choose between zoom chains, trigger re-grounding and, with an existence query, decide when to abstain; and (3) Compositional Structure Grounding, which composes spreadsheet cells from their headers. 73. 【2610.36873】RangeRectQuant: Compressing Scientific Fields for Every Future Rectangular Sum

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36873

作者：Jiahao Zhang,Xinling Wen,Yu Chen

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Scientific rasters, Scientific, Abstract, Independent pixel-error bounds, errors

备注： 10 pages, 6 figures. Code, data, core experiments and extended figures: [this https URL](https://github.com/Marchematics/RangeRectQuant)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Scientific rasters are often stored before the regions to be measured are known. Independent pixel-error bounds can force exact integer reconstruction when one absolute error budget covers every future rectangle. Coordinated changes allow every pixel to change while every rectangular sum remains accurate. A prefix sum accumulates values from the top-left corner. Four such sums determine any rectangular total. RangeRectQuant preserves the pixel range and designated exact samples while controlling errors in prefix sums. At each row position, we characterize exactly which errors let the remaining pixels meet these constraints. These errors form a single integer interval, even when many completions exist. A backward pass finds its endpoints; a forward pass uses them to choose quantized pixels. The resulting field meets the prescribed error budget for every axis-aligned rectangle, with linear work and workspace proportional to one row. Experiments on geographically held-out built-surface and water-occurrence maps show substantial storage savings over alternatives meeting the same requirements. Data producers encode once; later users measure arbitrary rectangles without the source, re-encoding, or query-specific correction.

备注： Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Open-Set Test-Time Adaptation (OSTTA) operates on non-stationary target streams where covariate and semantic shifts coexist. Existing TTA methods often obtain adaptation signals by updating model parameters or maintaining mutable memories, which can be costly and vulnerable to unknown samples. We present TALOR, a lightweight, backward-free rectification module that uses the source linear head as a geometric anchor. The head-induced basis decomposes each normalized feature into high-gain principal coordinates and low-gain tail coordinates. TALOR estimates a soft-routed affine correction in the principal subspace, combining a regime-level principal bias with a tail-conditioned slope that captures sample-level tail-to-principal coupling. Using weighted ridge regression, it subtracts this drift estimate from the principal coordinates, reconstructs the feature and feeds it into the source head. On the ImageNet-C benchmark with six csOOD datasets, TALOR outperforms the next-best UniEnt by 2.2% H-score while running 2.5x faster and using only 12% of its GPU memory; as a post-correction plug-in, it further boosts COME to 68.4% H-score with only 2% additional memory overhead.

Abstract:Real-to-Sim-to-Real (Real2Sim2Real) enables structured world representations for planning, interpretability, and safe execution. 76. 【2610.36858】Forget the Data, Keep the Capability: Switchable Unlearning for Retrieval

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36858

作者：Jingong Chen,Kyle Gao,Guangyuan Li,Chulwoo Pack,Sijia Liu,Kwanghee Won

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Privacy compliance requires, compliance requires re-identification, requires re-identification models, selectively forget individuals, Privacy compliance

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Privacy compliance requires re-identification models to selectively forget individuals while preserving their ability to recognize others. For this, we present MoME, a switchable framework that can adapt pre-trained backbones for privacy compliance, allowing the same model to switch between Full mode for standard inference and Core mode for unlearned inference. MoME splits one backbone into core parameters that preserve retrieval and zero-initialized parasitic parameters that carry the full-data knowledge, and training them under gradient isolation so the core never receives a forget-set gradient. Disabling the branch yields Core, which satisfies a request to delete a person's training data, and enabling it yields Full, which retains what the deleted data taught, so honoring the request is a flag flip rather than a retraining run. We prove Core independent of the forget set up to an audited set of coupling channels and introduce the rank-based Forgetting-Rank statistic to audit that independence in a trained model. On Market-1501, VeRi-776 and RegDB, Core reads at a retrained reference under three membership attacks while retrieving above it, and the retrieval advantage carries to ResNet and CLIP backbones. 77. 【2610.36857】Evaluating AlphaEarth Embeddings for Crop Classification: Separability, Temporal Dynamics, and Cross-Regional Transferability

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36857

作者：Priyanka Goyal,Maitreyee Sar,Adway Mitra,Manjira Sinha

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：Earth Observation, pixel-level spectral analysis, dimensions remains limited, based agricultural monitoring, domain-generalization dimensions remains

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Earth Observation (EO)-based agricultural monitoring is shifting from pixel-level spectral analysis toward representation-driven modeling, yet systematic evaluation of learned representations across spatial, temporal, and domain-generalization dimensions remains limited. Geospatial foundation models generate high-dimensional embeddings encoding spatio-temporal land-surface properties, but their utility for agricultural applications remains insufficiently understood. This study presents a representation-agnostic evaluation framework integrating geometric separability, temporal dynamics, spatial robustness, and cross-regional generalisation. A 64-dimensional AlphaEarth embedding space is benchmarked against Sentinel-2 reflectance, NDVI-derived phenology, and harmonic features using field-scale data from the Conterminous United States (CONUS; 2017-2023) and Northern India (2021). However, harmonic and NDVI features show stronger cross-continental transferability (US $\to$ India Macro F1: 0.493 vs. 0.326 for embeddings), reflecting better distributional alignment. The combined feature space yields balanced performance, supporting hybrid modeling. These findings reveal a fundamental trade-off between representational richness and cross-region transferability, providing a structured framework for feature selection in large-scale crop classification.

Abstract:Evaluation of Tamil handwritten character recognition (HCR) has largely focused on clean test images, and a standardized multi-corruption robustness benchmark for isolated Tamil HCR has not been established. We introduce uTHCD-C, a corruption-robustness benchmark based on the unconstrained Tamil Handwritten Character Database (uTHCD). The benchmark defines ten corruption types, including a handwriting-specific stroke-thinning corruption, at five severity levels. We evaluate nine convolutional and transformer-based architectures using five-fold stratified cross-validation and writer-independent testing. The accompanying efficiency measurements characterize the trade-offs between corruption robustness and computational requirements. By providing standardized corruption configurations, evaluation protocols, and comparative results, uTHCD-C establishes a reproducible basis for future research on robust and computationally efficient Tamil HCR.

80. 【2610.36844】AxisQ: Depth-Adaptive Channel--Frequency Quantization for Camouflaged Object Detection

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36844

作者：Chi Ruan,Tianqi Li,Xin He,Xue Geng,Ming-Ming Cheng,Yun Liu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Camouflaged object detection, Camouflaged object, aggressive post-training quantization, object detection, depends on weak

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Camouflaged object detection (COD) depends on weak texture and boundary cues that can be destroyed by aggressive post-training quantization (PTQ). Existing PTQ methods mainly optimize quantizers or reconstruct network blocks, while using a single precision-allocation domain throughout hierarchical backbones. Instead, we introduce AxisQ, a depth-adaptive bit-allocation framework motivated by the observation that the useful allocation axis changes with feature depth. Under a fixed per-layer average activation-bit budget, AxisQ allocates precision in the channel domain for shallow features and in the local-frequency domain for deep features, using COD-aware sensitivity to better preserve subtle foreground regions and ambiguous boundaries. We also formulate a boundary-aware Hessian approximation for reconstruction by incorporating boundary-weighted gradient and error statistics. Experiments across multiple COD models and diverse benchmarks demonstrate improved accuracy over the strongest baselines at low bit widths, with relative weighted F-measure gains of 12.7% for CFRN and 33.3% for CamoFormer-P on CAMO under W2A2 quantization. These results support adapting the allocation domain to feature depth as a transferable quantization strategy for hierarchical COD models.

The correction is needed in evaluation. In our 3B GRPO study, crediting equivalent clicks in the reward gives no stable gain over box rewards across three seeds and four test sets. We release the probe, the outcome maps, the audit and every prediction.

84. 【2610.36823】APESTRY: A Single Backbone for Video Generation and Robot Control

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36823

作者：Jeremy A. Collins,Seungjae Lee,Rachanon Wachakorn,Krishnan Srinivasan,Animesh Garg,Vitor Campagnolo Guizilini,Paarth Shah

类目：Robotics (cs.RO); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

备注： Accepted to Conference on Robot Learning (CoRL) 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Pretrained video generation models encode rich priors about physical interactions, but leveraging them for robot control typically requires either resource-intensive video generation at test time or action heads trained from scratch that do not fully utilize the pretrained backbone. We introduce Tokenizing Actions as Video Patches in a Shared Transformer Policy (TAPESTRY), a world action model that repurposes a pretrained video diffusion transformer into a robotic policy without requiring video generation at inference. Our key idea is to treat robot actions as single-patch tokens in the video model's latent space. A lightweight linear projection maps each action timestep to the patch dimensions of the pretrained VAE, and the resulting tokens are processed by the same patchifying layers and transformer backbone used for video. The shared backbone is optimized through three diffusion objectives sampled per minibatch element: Video diffusion uses VAE latents from robot and human videos to establish dynamics priors. Joint video+action diffusion trains on robot demonstrations using a coupled diffusion timestep and an asymmetric attention mask. Action-only diffusion denoises actions conditioned on clean image latents. At test time, the model generates a compact action token sequence in a single DDIM step from pure noise, producing smooth action chunks at 84 ms per action chunk on consumer hardware and avoiding the cost of full video generation. Across simulation benchmarks and real-robot experiments, TAPESTRY achieves strong performance with substantially lower inference cost than comparable joint video--action policies. More information can be found at this https URL.

Abstract:Multimodal large language models have made substantial progress in visual question answering, but complex visual reasoning often requires locating and comparing multiple pieces of question-relevant visual evidence. Existing methods that move intermediate reasoning into the visual latent space usually follow a single linear reasoning trajectory, which can be limiting when multiple visual hypotheses or competing evidence regions are relevant. We propose Latent Visual Contrastive Reasoning, a branch-aware latent reasoning framework that generates multiple question-conditioned latent visual branches as soft hypotheses over image tokens. 86. 【2610.36793】Visual Autoregressive Generation with Next Token Imagination

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36793

作者：Ruihang Jiang,Zongyao Zhao,Zhaolin Wang,Yuanwei Liu,Xiatian Zhu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：content is enormous, arguably unbounded, visual, token, visual content

备注： 19 pages, 7 figures. Code and checkpoints: [this https URL](https://github.com/JackRuihang/NTI)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:The space of possible visual content is enormous, and arguably unbounded. Any training set, however large, therefore covers only a tiny fraction of it. Yet generative models are typically trained to fit only the images they are given. In visual autoregressive (AR) generation, for example, each token is predicted from the preceding ground-truth (GT) tokens and supervised solely by the GT next token. Confining learning to observed data in this way limits how much of the visual distribution a model can capture. In this work, we hypothesize that letting a model imagine beyond its training data, by exploring plausible visual realizations it has never observed, can ease this limitation. We use visual AR generation as a proof of concept, since its fixed, discrete token space makes imagination easier to steer and constrain. We propose Next Token Imagination (NTI), a method that allows the model to explore beyond the GT next token, subject to a carefully designed criterion that keeps this imagination bounded by the underlying visual distribution. Extensive experiments on ImageNet show that NTI substantially and consistently improves the fidelity of generated images, approaching state-of-the-art diffusion and AR models while using a smaller model and lower training cost.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36785

作者：Rangya Zhang,Jiaping Xiao,Yujing Shang,Yuhang Zhang,Mingsheng Li,Mir Feroskhan

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Multi-view tracking extends visual coverage beyond a single camera, providing complementary observations when targets are occluded or leave the view. This raises a question: how can observations from different views be matched to the correct identities without offline scene-specific training or camera calibration? Progress toward this goal remains limited because many offline formulations assume such deployment-specific knowledge, while online adaptation can amplify association errors. We study this problem as online multi-view identity maintenance (OMIM): targets are initialized once per view, with no further identity labels provided after each initialization. To this end, we introduce OmniGlimpse, an asymmetric propose-verify framework that separates adaptive association from stable identity consolidation. At the representation level, short-term local and cross-view evidence is mapped into target-specific adaptive spaces to propose associations, whereas long-term identity distributions are maintained for verification in a canonical space defined by a frozen encoder. At the update level, short-term evidence may guide current associations, whereas, after bootstrapping, only verified observations may update persistent memory or drive adaptation. Under a unified target-specified evaluation protocol, OmniGlimpse improves over the best evaluated online baseline for each metric by 9.1 IDF1 and 22.1 CVIDF1 points on M3Track. The corresponding gains on MvMHAT are 20.3 and 26.2 points. Despite using no offline scene-specific training, it outperforms offline-trained references in IDF1 and remains competitive in cross-view association. These results support an approach to online identity maintenance that adapts to changing appearances while consolidating reliable evidence into stable identity anchors.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Feed-forward Gaussian Splatting enables efficient novel view synthesis, but pixel-aligned prediction makes the primitive count grow with image resolution and view count, leading to substantial redundancy and increased computational cost. Non-pixel-aligned methods address this limitation by predicting a fixed number of Gaussians. Without explicit ray and depth constraints, these methods yield misplaced Gaussian centers, causing rendering artifacts under large viewpoint changes. To address this issue, we introduce GeoGSplat, a geometry-grounded feed-forward model that explicitly incorporates geometric priors into the non-pixel-aligned framework. GeoGSplat surpasses both state-of-the-art pixel-aligned and non-pixel-aligned Gaussian Splatting methods on DTU and NRGBD in terms of novel view synthesis and reconstruction. However, the dense multi-object distribution and complex backgrounds in RS imagery make it difficult to simultaneously achieve fine-grained cross-modal alignment and efficient retrieval. Existing methods either rely on complex cross-modal interactions that lead to low retrieval efficiency, or depend on large-scale vision-language adaptation, which requires massive data and computational resources. To address these issues, we develop a fast-then-fine (FTF) retrieval framework centered on interaction-free multi-granular recall and parameter-free text-guided cross-modal reranking with the Balanced Text-guided Interaction Block (BTIB). Specifically, interaction-free multi-granular recall pre-computes gallery embeddings and matches them with the corresponding query embedding without full-gallery token-level text-image interaction, while text-guided cross-modal reranking with BTIB refines local semantic correspondence within the top-K candidate set. Furthermore, an inter- and intra-modal loss is designed to jointly optimize cross-modal alignment across multi-granular representations. Extensive experiments on public benchmarks demonstrate that the FTF achieves competitive retrieval accuracy while significantly improving retrieval efficiency compared with existing methods.

93. 【2610.36750】Multi-task Disentangling Fusion Mamba-CNN for MODIS Spectral-Temporal Unmixing with Endmember Variability Modeling

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36750

作者：Motasem Alkayid,Yimin Zhu,Naser El-Sheimy,Lincoln Linlin Xu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：inter-class signature ambiguity, remains challenging due, naively fusing multiple, multiple expert representations, spectral library construction

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Spectral-temporal unmixing of MODIS time series is essential for applications such as abundance mapping and spectral library construction, yet it remains challenging due to (i) spectral-temporal information coupling in MODIS data, (ii) intra-class endmember spectral variability and inter-class signature ambiguity, and (iii) the instability of naively fusing multiple expert representations. This paper presents the Multi- task Disentangling Fusion Mamba (MDF-Mamba) to address these limitations. Convolutional modules each process a single spectral channel, while the Mamba branch encodes the cross-channel seasonal trajectory, forming a multi-branch expert ensemble with soft inter-expert reweighting based on squeeze-and-excitation. Finally, we design a multi-task training objective that combines supervised abundance estimation with self-supervised spectral reconstruction and endmember regularization terms on the same training data, yielding a highly efficient framework for spectral-temporal unmixing. Experiments on MODIS time series covering the Alberta and Saskatchewan provinces show that MDF-Mamba achieves the best performance on 12 of 14 evaluation metrics across both datasets (7 of 7 on Alberta; 5 of 7 on Saskatchewan), ranking second on the remaining two.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36719

99. 【2610.36671】Look Only When Needed: Certificate-Guided Visual Routing for Efficient Diffusion VLMs

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36671

作者：Donghyeon Cho,Joowon Kim,Seungho Shin,Eunho Yang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：Diffusion vision-language models, Diffusion vision-language, image, vision-language models, Visual token pruning

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Diffusion vision-language models (DVLMs) process their entire input at every denoising step. Since visual tokens make up 89-97% of this input on the benchmarks we study, most of the decoding computation is spent repeatedly processing the same image. Visual token pruning methods partially relieve this burden by removing part of the visual tokens, but they lose the removed information and still process the remaining image at every step. To measure how often the image is actually needed, we compare LLaDA-V's predictions with and without the image at every decoding step and find that removing the image changes a token being unmasked at only 22.8% of steps on average and at as few as 7.9%. We therefore propose LOWN, a decoding method that decides at every step whether to process the image and how many tokens to unmask. LOWN predicts the masked tokens without the image and processes the image only when this prediction disagrees with the last image-conditioned prediction. Agreement certifies the earlier prediction for reuse, and the number of certified tokens sets how many are unmasked in that step. We further observe that the need for the image differs across benchmarks, so LOWN's adaptive decision is needed rather than a fixed schedule. Without training or a tuned threshold, LOWN retains 99.6% of LLaDA-V's score on ten benchmarks at 35.3% of the decoding FLOPs on average. LOWN also generalizes across LLaDA-based DVLMs, composes orthogonally with visual token pruning, and runs under key-value caching for further efficiency gains. Code and project page are available at this https URL

100. 【2610.36665】Haste Makes Waste? Towards Safe Few-Step Text-to-Image Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36665

作者：Lior Ziv,Mahmood Sharif

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Cryptography and Security (cs.CR)

关键词：gained traction due, rates remain unknown, fast generation times, remain unknown, few-step models

备注： Accepted at IEEE SP 2027

点击查看摘要

Abstract:While few-step distilled text-to-image models have gained traction due to their fast generation times, the speed-up implications on their unsafe generation rates remain unknown. We uncover an inherent tension between speed and safety: few-step models are often more unsafe than their teachers, and reducing the number of inference steps consistently raises unsafe generation rates. We further find that acceleration makes unsafe concepts harder to erase in few-step models, with existing concept-erasure methods markedly less effective than in teachers. We offer a mechanistic diagnosis for why erasure methods fail on few-step models, finding that the fragility of few-step models' generation process and the way generation guidance is usually distilled into those models may hinder established concept-erasure methods. To address the speed-safety trade-off, we propose DICE (Distillation-Integrated Concept Erasure), a post-distillation erasure method tailored for few-step models. DICE aligns both its latent construction and its generation-preservation loss with the distillation method that produced the model, keeping erasure faithful to few-step models. DICE demonstrates strong results in our evaluation. For example, on FLUX Schnell, DICE reduces unsafe-generation rate to 0.046 (vs. at least 0.161 for prior erasure methods we test), while mostly preserving benign generation quality.

101. 【2610.36662】SWIM: Learning What Matters from Behaviors for JEPA World Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36662

作者：Jiaming Hu,Shi Bo,Yan Zheng,Tian Wang,Florian Dubost,Alejandro Mottini,Junze Liu,Arvind Srinivasan,Kai Zhong,Kun Qian,Sharon Gao,Vianne Gao,Houyu Zhang,Qingjun Cui

类目：Robotics (cs.RO); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Joint-embedding predictive architectures, Joint-embedding predictive, enable world models, predictive architectures, state

备注： 25 pages, 1 figure

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) enable world models to plan from images by predicting future embeddings. However, their visual prediction objectives do not explicitly incorporate task-relevant state information, so the physical state that determines task progress may not be reliably captured in the latent space. Supervising world models with simulator state can make planning more informative, but it requires deciding which state features matter. Features are typically selected by hand or weighted uniformly, and a uniform weighting gives every standardized state feature equal influence, so redundant coordinates can overcount the same variation, while irrelevant ones can obscure task-relevant differences. To address this limitation, we propose SWIM (State Weighting with Inverse-Dynamics Model), which learns state weights from offline trajectories by predicting recorded actions from current states and observed future displacements. The learned weights quantify each feature's contribution to action prediction and define a weighted state metric for state-supervised training, which we demonstrate by aligning latent and state distances. The inverse-dynamics model is used only during training, so the resulting world model plans from images with no additional planning-time computation. Across five simulated robotic manipulation tasks, SWIM consistently improves planning success over uniform state weighting.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36658

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Point cloud registration is an essential task for scene reconstruction, requiring the alignment of partially overlapping point clouds. Under low-overlap conditions, this task remains challenging because existing geometry-only methods cannot distinguish geometrically similar but semantically distinct structures (e.g., TV corners vs. desk corners and chair legs vs. table legs), resulting in incorrect correspondences. To address this geometric ambiguity, we present Semantic-Augmented Geometric Experts (SAGE), which integrates frozen pre-trained point cloud semantic representations into the registration pipeline without requiring additional sensors or fine-tuning the semantic encoder. To effectively leverage these semantic distinctions, SAGE employs a Semantic-Guided Mixture of Experts (SG-MoE) architecture. Through semantic-aware routing, SG-MoE enables each expert to specialize in distinct semantic-geometric patterns, reducing mismatches that confound geometry-only methods. With a structure-aware estimator, SAGE achieves the highest registration recall on 3DLoMatch while remaining competitive on 3DMatch. When evaluated with the same number of refinement iterations, SAGE further achieves the highest inlier ratio and registration recall on both benchmarks. These results indicate that frozen semantic representations provide effective cues for resolving geometric ambiguity in low-overlap registration.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36624

Abstract:World Action Models (WAMs) leverage pretrained video generative models for robotic control by jointly predicting future visual observations and actions. The visual prediction objective plays a key role in adapting pretrained video representations to the target robot domain. Yet standard visual prediction inherited from video pretraining treats all visual regions uniformly, despite their differing relevance to robotic control. We therefore investigate the spatial allocation of visual supervision in WAMs and show that focusing supervision on dynamic regions can substantially improve policy performance. Building on this finding, we introduce Motion-Guided Asymmetric Prediction (MoGAP), which augments WAM training with motion-focused visual prediction. MoGAP is trained alongside standard video-action prediction through a training-only auxiliary prediction path within the shared video diffusion backbone. Across robot manipulation benchmarks and WAM architectures, MoGAP consistently improves policy performance, increasing success rates on the GR-1 tabletop benchmark from 38.8% to 47.5% for Fast-WAM (Wan2.2-5B) and from 52.3% to 62.3% for Cosmos3-Nano.

104. 【2610.36610】4D Reconstruction with Events RGB

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36610

作者：Dulanga Weerakoon,Darshana Rathnayake,Van Minh Do,Hemanth Sabbella,Kaichen Zhou,Fangneng Zhan,Alok Prakash,Sanjay E. Sarma,Archan Misra,Paul Pu Liang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：state-of the-art feed-forward, the-art feed-forward methods, feed-forward methods rely, methods rely solely, Reconstructing dynamic

备注： Accepted at NeVi 2026 workshop at the 19th European Conference on Computer Vision (ECCV 2026)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Reconstructing dynamic 3D scenes over time (4D reconstruction) from a single moving camera is central to robotics and AR/VR applications, yet state-of the-art feed-forward methods rely solely on RGB frames and therefore inherit the failure modes of conventional cameras, including motion blur and temporal aliasing under fast or non-uniform camera and object motion. Event cameras report per-pixel brightness changes asynchronously at microsecond resolution, offering a complementary signal that is largely immune to these artifacts, but whether this modality can be incorporated into existing RGB based 4D reconstruction pipelines without a full architectural redesign remains an open question. In this preliminary study, we investigate a minimal extension: we augment Monst3R, an existing RGB-only 4D reconstruction model, with an additional event-camera input branch, leaving the rest of the architecture unchanged. Across 3 benchmark datasets, this simple RGB-event fusion reduces Abs Rel by 3.4% on average relative to the RGB-only Monst3R baseline, and a sensitivity analysis shows that the improvement grows with the magnitude of camera and object motion, with the largest gains observed under high object motion conditions where RGB-only reconstruction degrades most. These early results suggest that even a lightweight, non-invasive fusion of event data can meaningfully improve 4D reconstruction robustness, motivating a more thorough architectural investigation in future work.
