106. 【2610.36593】WithinVAE: Compact Visual Autoencoding with Self-Distilled Semantics

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36593

作者：Yuzhou Ji,Jiepeng Wang,Tianyu Jin,Lizhuang Ma,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Xuelong Li

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：balance reconstruction fidelity, semantic, semantic structure, visual autoencoder, reconstruction fidelity

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Visual autoencoders must balance reconstruction fidelity, compactness, and semantic structure. Existing semantic tokenizers typically inherit semantics from pretrained recognition encoders, whose invariances can discard the appearance detail required for inversion. We show that this inheritance is unnecessary: semantic structure can be learned inside an autoencoder's own reconstructive representation. We introduce WithinVAE, a compact variational visual autoencoder with a sequential bottleneck, DINO/iBOT-style self-distillation from an exponential-moving-average copy of the autoencoder itself, and position-dependent token masking to achieve a 4-fold compression ratio, which is free of pretrained representation encoder or external semantic target. On ImageNet-1K at $256\times256$, WithinVAE improves all four reported reconstruction metrics over RPiAE including PSNR by 1.36 dB, while using half its latent scalar capacity. Under frozen evaluation, the same latents improve linear probing by 6.5 points over RPiAE and 5.1 points over our reconstruction-only baseline, attributing the semantic gains to self-distillation rather than architecture or external supervision. An auxiliary latent-to-latent model reproduces the ordered-compression trend, and a pixel-to-latent model transfers the objective to a pretrained generative space. These results separate the origin of semantic supervision from its use: a compact visual autoencoder can organize semantic structure without borrowing a recognition representation.

108. 【2610.36569】Decoupling Consistency and Conflict across MLLM Layers for Fake News Detection

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36569

作者：Kai Qian,Dongjie Xu,Weijie Shi,Jiaqi Wang,Haolun Ma,Jia Zhu,Jiajie Xu

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：Web content faces, trustworthiness of Web, Web content, faces a growing, growing threat

备注： 12 pages

点击查看摘要

Abstract:The trustworthiness of Web content faces a growing threat from multimodal fake news. Real news usually shows aligned image-text semantics, while fake news often contains a few conflicts at varying levels, such as mismatches in objects, scenes, events, and claims. However, existing methods usually treat consistency and conflict in a coarse and static way, limiting their ability to capture sparse conflicts and adapt to varying conflict levels. Inspired by the distinct knowledge encoded across layers in large language models, we propose Multi-Level Consistency and Conflict Decoupling (MLCCD), which captures conflicts at different levels from different layers of multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Specifically, we first use the MLLM's final decision representation to select visual and textual tokens relevant to the task, preventing irrelevant content from diluting sparse conflicts. Then, we preserve tokens whose representations change sharply between adjacent layers, capturing the knowledge each layer adds. Finally, we decouple this knowledge into consistency and conflict evidence and aggregate each across layers with its own router, adapting to varying conflict levels. Experiments on three benchmarks show that MLCCD outperforms existing methods.

109. 【2610.36554】PRISM-BENCH: Diagnosing Grounded and Faithful Multimodal Reasoning Beyond Final-Answer Accuracy

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36554

作者：Vansh Bhatia,Mehdi Rezagholizadeh,Parsa Ashrafi Fashi,Igor Gilitschenski,Vikram Appia,Emad Barsoum

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：Final-answer accuracy reports, stated reasoning reflects, reflects that evidence, cost to produce, Final-answer accuracy

备注： Accepted as a poster at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Grounded and Faithful Vision-Language Models for Real-World Deployment (VLM4RWD). 18 pages, 10 figures, 7 tables. Dataset: [this https URL](https://huggingface.co/datasets/vanshbhatia/PRISM-BENCH)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Final-answer accuracy reports whether a vision-language model was right, but not whether its answer was grounded in the image, whether its stated reasoning reflects that evidence, or what the answer cost to produce. Those are the properties that decide whether a system can be deployed. We present PRISM-BENCH, a benchmark of 1,000 expert-curated questions spanning five under-represented reasoning categories, in which every item carries an idealized reasoning trace written by one human annotator and validated by another. The traces support two diagnostics that accuracy cannot provide. A perception probe, in which the model answers from a description it wrote itself, attributes roughly 85% of frontier-model failures to misperception and places the bottleneck at grounding. Trace alignment predicts answer correctness for all 25 systems tested (macro AUROC 0.66) and survives length control, so it measures reasoning content and not verbosity. We further introduce CORE, an efficiency-aware metric that reorders 24.2% of model pairs relative to accuracy while remaining uncorrelated with response length. Across 25 open and closed systems, including GPT-5 and Claude Opus 4.6, the best accuracy is 66.0%. On the four categories with a human baseline the best system trails humans by 18.4 points, and recent frontier gains fall on recognition and not on spatial grounding.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Unified conditional image generation requires a single model to preserve different aspects of a visual input while producing a target image that follows textual guidance. Pretrained video models provide a promising foundation by encoding visual transformations across time, yet existing approaches typically assign the condition and target to fixed temporal positions. 111. 【2610.36540】Frugal Handwritten Text Recognition for Sub-Saharan Civil-Status Registers: A Smartphone-to-Records Pipeline with CPU-Only Inference

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36540

作者：Ousmane Hamadou,Jean Louis Kedieng Ebongue Fendji

Abstract:Handwritten civil-status registers across Sub-Saharan Africa are being photographed, but rarely transcribed, which leaves their content unsearchable. These results are evidence of feasibility within one registry, not of performance on unseen writers or sites; we discuss these limitations in detail.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36533

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Pathology foundation models are typically benchmarked on classification performance at full label availability and on in-distribution test sets, while calibration, behavior at low label budgets, and robustness under cross-institutional shift are seldom evaluated jointly. (iii) Whether additional source-domain labels help or harm target calibration tracks how well the probe transfers: below a transfer AUROC of roughly 0.85, more labels worsen target ECE in 86-88% of combinations. 114. 【2610.36510】Identity or Evidence? Auditing Evidence Dependence in Agentic Life-Science Benchmarks

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36510

作者：Danai Brilli,Maria Georgila

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Agentic life-science benchmarks, Agentic life-science, life-science benchmarks give, recorded outcome, give an agent

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Agentic life-science benchmarks give an agent evidence about a named entity and score an answer against a recorded outcome. That score tests reasoning over evidence only if the evidence, rather than the name, determines the answer. We examine this distinction by varying identity and evidence independently on a clinical-outcome benchmark and published tasks from Biomni and LAB-Bench. Blinding identity matters little when a prompt contains a decisive sequence or passage. It matters a great deal when the prompt is mostly a name and candidate list (0.742 named versus 0.082 blinded; chance 0.093). Across 96 LAB-Bench LitQA2 questions, the gap increases under one fixed passage-redaction scheme (+0.000 to +0.135; per-instance slope +0.147, p= 0.004). On the clinical benchmark, a no-evidence recall probe matches prediction accuracy and recovers a database-specific programme count. So, we argue that benchmarks intended to measure evidence-dependent reasoning should report identity-blinded results, retrieval telemetry, and positive controls.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36509

关键词：Multimodal forgery detection, encountered after deployment, commonly studied, expected to approximate, approximate those encountered

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Multimodal forgery detection is commonly studied under a static assumption: training attacks are expected to approximate those encountered after deployment. This assumption becomes increasingly fragile as generators, editing systems, and multimodal large language models continually introduce new forgery mechanisms and forensic evidence. We formulate this problem as attack-incremental continual multimodal forgery detection, where the binary real/fake interface remains fixed while emerging attacks must be learned without forgetting previous ones. We introduce Fable-Bench, which organizes attack evolution through complementary joint-modality, text-side, and image-side protocols. We further propose ARISE (Authentic Reference-Informed Stability Enhancement), motivated by the open-ended forged class and the comparatively reusable geometry of authentic content. ARISE fits a real-only Gaussian mixture model to projected authentic replay representations as a compact historical reference. Meanwhile, the proposed Gaussian-Responsibility-Aware Stability Preservation (GRASP) derives a sample-adaptive anisotropic metric that strongly constrains historically stable representation directions while retaining plasticity elsewhere. Our Vulnerability-Aware Replay (VAR) further estimates stage-wise vulnerability from authentic-density degradation, forged-to-authentic boundary confusion, and authentic-mode occupancy drift, then assigns bounded replay emphasis to fragile stages. All adaptive signals are obtained from training-time replay statistics, while inference uses only the original multimodal backbone and binary classification head. Experiments across three attack-evolution protocols and multiple detector architectures demonstrate improved historical retention and final average performance over representative continual-learning baselines.

Abstract:Scanning Transmission Electron Microscopy (STEM) enables the observation of atomic arrangements at sub-angstrom resolution and plays a key role in the discovery and characterization of advanced materials. 118. 【2610.36467】Confident First, Reflective Second: Reinforcement Learning of Confidence-Preserving Reflection for Video Understanding

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36467

作者：Zihao Lin,Jingyuan Liu,Yucheng Zhang,Yuguang Yao,Lifu Huang

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Multimedia (cs.MM)

关键词：Video models answer, initial answer, decisive moment, Video models, sampled frames

备注： 18 pages, 8 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Video models answer from sampled frames and may miss the decisive moment. A common remedy is to commit to an answer, re-examine the video, and confirm or revise it, a loop now mainly learned with reinforcement learning (RL). We find that RL erodes the initial answer, a phenomenon we term confidence erosion, where confidence means the label-verified reliability of the initial answer instead of self-reported certainty: because errors can be repaired later, a correct initial answer earns less reward, and the initial answer stalls even as the final answer's gain over it rises. We propose the principle confident first, reflective second: the initial answer must stay reliable on its own, above a floor set by a reference policy, and reflection may only add to it. Our method, CoFire, implements this principle with one mechanism for each erosion channel: reward incentives, parameter drift, and gradient interference. CoFire reaches 67.0% mean accuracy on Video-MME, MLVU, and LongVideoBench, 1.7 points above the strongest compared 7B method, and improves initial-answer and final accuracy over outcome-reward RL by 4.1 and 3.7 points on average: a stronger initial answer carries through to a stronger final one.

Abstract:We study 3D visual grounding in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), where a referring expression should identify a target object as both a rendered 2D mask and a set of explicit 3D Gaussians. 121. 【2610.36460】Full Collapse Is Suboptimal: Controlled Partial Neural Collapse for Long-Tailed Recognition

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36460

作者：Hyeontaek Hwang,Cheonghwan Hur,Daeyoung Kim

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：Equiangular Tight Frame, fixed Equiangular Tight, Recent long-tailed recognition, Tight Frame, Equiangular Tight

备注： Accepted at WACV 2027

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Recent long-tailed recognition (LTR) methods incorporate neural collapse (NC) and align features to a fixed Equiangular Tight Frame (ETF) as a uniform target for all classes. However, forcing tail classes to fully collapse over-contracts their samples and weakens generalization. To address this, we propose a controlled partial neural collapse, realized through two coordinated controls on a backbone: the Class-Adaptive Sparse Activation (CASA) classifier and the ETF-constrained adaptive margin (ETF-AM) loss. Rather than forcing a uniform full collapse, this regime allows head classes to collapse along the ETF while tail classes retain within-class diversity. This prevents the tail from being forced into full collapse, which is suboptimal under imbalance. To set the dimensionality of collapse, CASA induces class-related node specialization through feature-conditioned gating so that tail classes occupy their own feature directions. For the clearance axis, ETF-AM applies a frequency-adaptive margin, with smaller margins for head classes and larger for tail classes. On CIFAR100-LT, ImageNet-LT, Places-LT, and iNaturalist-2018, our method achieves the best overall accuracy over prior methods. In the empirical analysis, within-class spread increases monotonically from head to tail classes, consistent with tail classes retaining variability rather than fully collapsing.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36449

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Do vision-language models (VLMs) share our ability to communicate visually? We share preliminary findings, starting from a surprising observation: simple visual symbols like a red heart can strongly influence model behavior on seemingly unrelated tasks, such as steering models towards recursive solutions on simple coding problems. Notably, this steering effect is largely implicit, as models' direct interpretations and thinking traces about visual symbols diverge substantially from their observed behavior. In further communication games involving abstract concepts and stories, models can creatively use visuals to transmit meaning to one another, although some communications remain non-intuitive to humans. These findings reveal that VLMs might be capable of rich, creative, and visually-grounded communication, but their interpretation and production of visual meaning diverges from human conventions in surprising ways. Together this work highlights both the promise and challenges of visual communication between humans and VLMs.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:We present Scalable Pixel-anchored End-to-end Diffusion (SpeeDiff), a latent diffusion method that jointly trains the VAE and the diffusion model from scratch. However, a naive end-to-end implementation severely degrades performance, as unrestricted backpropagation of the diffusion loss leads to latent space collapse. Our main technical contribution is a simple yet effective Tweedie Pixel Reconstruction (TPR) loss, which provides additional pixel-level feedback by decoding a predicted clean latent from an intermediate noisy state using Tweedie's formula, thereby alleviating collapse. 备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:We tackle the challenge of building real-time interactive video worlds with user-defined behaviors. Such worlds must track object states, apply interaction rules, and render the resulting consequences as users act. Yet existing end-to-end video world models typically learn interaction dynamics within the generator, making it difficult to specify object-level rules and reason about their consequences. In this work, we introduce BoxWorld, which separates interaction reasoning from generative rendering. Given user-specified rules, a coding agent creates a Box World Engine that determines interaction outcomes and exposes the resulting object states through a lightweight interface of layout boxes, persistent object attributes, and dynamic status variables, leaving appearance and physical effects to generative rendering. A Box-State Generative Renderer then renders this evolving state stream into video autoregressively, enabling online control over object motion, state switches, and conditional events. To this end, we introduce a three-stage training strategy that learns object grounding from open-domain videos and state transitions from gameplay videos, both annotated automatically, and finally performs rollout distillation on self-generated histories. We further propose PairedStateBench, a paired benchmark for fine-grained interaction control. BoxWorld substantially outperforms end-to-end world models and remains competitive across multiple public benchmarks. Project page: this https URL.

Abstract:Crowd-counting models deployed on edge devices face two challenges: inputs are degraded along the capture and transmission pipeline, while models are often quantized to satisfy resource constraints of the edge. 132. 【2610.36337】CRLoMTL: Conflict-Resolution through Low-rank matrices for Multi-Task Learning

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36337

作者：Ahmed Agiza,Tasneem Shaffee,Rahil Badkul,Sherief Reda

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Multi-task learning, related vision tasks, improves model efficiency, efficiency by sharing, sharing representations

备注： Accepted at the British Machine Vision Conference (BMVC 2026)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Multi-task learning (MTL) improves model efficiency by sharing representations across related vision tasks, but it often suffers from negative transfer when task updates interfere with one another. Existing conflict-mitigation methods typically operate in gradient space, requiring explicit task-gradient manipulation and additional optimization steps. We propose Conflict-Resolution through Low-Rank Matrices for Multi-Task Learning (CRLoMTL), a parameter-efficient framework that mitigates task interference in the low-rank parameter-update space. CRLoMTL learns task-specific LoRA updates through task-specific passes, where each task updates its own low-rank matrices, and integrates these updates through global shared-subspace fusion. Specifically, we introduce a shared-subspace resolver based on singular value decomposition (SVD), referred to as the SVD resolver, which extracts a common low-dimensional orthonormal basis across task adapters and projects task updates into this shared space before merging them into a single deployment-time update. The resulting update can be folded into the backbone, preserving the inference structure of the original model without additional runtime branches. Experiments with Swin Transformer backbones on PASCAL-Context, NYUD-v2, and CelebA show that CRLoMTL achieves a favorable accuracy-parameter trade-off, improving over single-task baselines by up to 2.77% on PASCAL-Context, 5.03% on NYUD-v2, and 3.50% on CelebA, while training only a small fraction of the parameters used by single-task models.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36269

作者：Donghao Zeng(1),Nishant Subramani(2),Tamar Rott Shaham(3),Benno Krojer(1 and 4) ((1) Mila, McGill University, (2) Carnegie Mellon University, (3) MIT CSAIL, (4) Northeastern University)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Are concepts and mechanisms unified across modalities in current AI models, or instead implemented separately for each one? We operationalize this broad question of modality unification by studying cross-modal steering: 1) finding vectors that successfully steer generation on tokens of one modality, which then 2) transfer zero-shot to steer generation when added to tokens of another modality. Our study goes beyond simple correlational alignment (e.g. representational similarity) because steering is fundamentally causal. We study cross-modal steering in several unified multimodal models (UMMs), since they possess shared parameters for language and image generation, focusing on these two modalities. Across diverse steered concepts such as emotion and object size, we show that steering vectors transfer zero-shot in both directions (text-to-image image-to-text). Even for concepts that are highly unique to one modality (e.g. formal vs. colloquial language), steering vectors often induce a consistent, interpretable effect in the other modality, such as formal clothing and design. This transfer has a geometric signature too: despite a clear modality gap separating text and image representations, steering vectors with strong cross-modal transferability tend to point in similar directions for the same concept. Our findings shed light on how vision and language interact inside unified multimodal models and point towards more general, modality-agnostic approaches for controlling their behavior.

Abstract:Dyadic relationship recognition aims to identify the latent social relationship between two individuals from their multimodal interactions. Existing methods typically project heterogeneous cues into a shared representation and optimise a flat classification objective, overlooking that relationships may rely on different interaction patterns. For instance, distant relationships can often be inferred from direct behavioural correspondence, whereas close relationships are better reflected by coordinated affective, behavioural, and multimodal responses. To address this limitation, we propose HiRe-DRR, a Hierarchical Multimodal Interaction Reasoning framework that organises relationship evidence from three complementary perspectives: feature level, interaction type, and relationship hierarchy. Abstract:Long-video question answering is challenging since the answer often hinges on a few decisive moments scattered throughout the video, while memory constraints force Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to sample frames at extremely low rates, resulting in sparsity that risks omitting key evidence. Though aggressively enlarging the context size is intuitive, we argue that performance remains fundamentally constrained by the bias, \emph{i.e.}, the misleading cues caused by spurious \textit{linguistic} correlations and salient yet irrelevant \textit{visual} observations. In this paper, we first introduce {S-MME}, a benchmark designed to systematically diagnose shortcut-biased behavior in MLLMs. To mitigate this failure, we further propose a training-free framework, Counterfactual Long-video Evidence-Aware Reasoning (CLEAR). By treating the full video-question pair as a complete view, CLEAR estimates bias effects via augmented views by only keeping the question or locally salient visual clips. Then, we intervene hidden states to mitigate the discrepancy at inference such that the correct answer can be predicted confidently. With experimental studies across five benchmarks, we show that our method is generalizable and can be integrated in different MLLMs, yielding consistent gains in prediction and robustness. We hope CLEAR can offer a complementary direction in test-time scaling for long-video understanding.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Monocular 3D human mesh recovery (HMR) remains unreliable under weak observations caused by long camera-to-person distances, occlusion, and motion blur. In challenging scenarios such as sports, these factors make the problem highly ambiguous, as different 3D poses may yield similar 2D projections. This ambiguity is especially severe for one-stage HMR, which recovers all meshes directly from the full image without person crops or external 2D cues. To handle this ambiguity, we present UT-HMR ( Uncertainty-guided Temporal HMR), to our knowledge the first one-stage HMR framework to capture correlated pose uncertainty across joints. Each person query predicts a Gaussian distribution over the full SMPL pose with a compact low-rank-plus-diagonal covariance, which is converted into joint-wise precision weights for heteroscedastic supervision while keeping inference deterministic. Temporal early fusion further injects neighboring-frame evidence before pose decoding, supplying the visual evidence needed to resolve the ambiguity that the uncertainty captures. With a lightweight ViT-B backbone, UT-HMR achieves state-of-the-art results on VIP-HockeyPose-v2 (ice hockey) and WorldPose (soccer), reducing MPJPE, PA-MPJPE, and PVE by up to 4.7%, 3.8%, and 5.6%, and remains competitive on 3DPW against methods with larger backbones and higher input resolutions.

139. 【2610.36192】Beyond the Estimate: Wiener-Informed Decoding for Easier Training of Pixel-Space Flow Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36192

作者：Ziqiao Wang,Yang You

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：Learning velocity fields, velocity fields directly, difficult and slow, fields directly, space is difficult

备注： 23 pages

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Learning velocity fields directly in pixel space is difficult and slow. We show that coarse second-order image statistics can ease this task: frozen Wiener estimates improve velocity learning, with gains extending beyond the linear predictions they explicitly provide. Moreover, we observe similar but complementary gains from frozen bases and pixel decoding. These observations motivate Wiener-informed Decoding (WiD), which uses statistical knowledge both for direct prediction and to organize decoder computation without modifying native blocks. WiD combines a trainable Wiener prediction path with coefficient interfaces that inject features into the native decoder and return its predicted coefficient corrections, with these components initialized with data statistics. All introduced parameters, including the spectrum and coordinate transforms, are trained jointly under the original pixel-space velocity objective. Across model scales and decoder architectures, WiD accelerates training and improves generation quality with modest parameter overhead. It also yields smoother gradient trajectories and remains effective with representation alignment. WiD improves class-conditional and text-to-image generation across different resolutions. On ImageNet 256 x 256, PixelDiT-XL with WiD reaches FID 1.53 after 500 epochs, achieving comparable quality to PixelDiT with 37.5% fewer training epochs. Statistical denoisers contribute beyond the estimate: their computational structure can also ease pixel-space training.

Abstract:3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has rapidly become the preferred representation for high-fidelity novel view synthesis and 3D scene reconstruction. 备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Geometry foundation models recover camera poses and dense geometry from a set of images with a feed-forward network, but they expect pinhole images. That assumption is inside the backbone too: the positional encoding and the training loss both bind an image position to a direction in space, and both depend on the lens. We condition both on the dense ray field, the unit direction through every pixel, which describes any central camera without a lens type, distortion coefficients or a bound on field of view. Angular coordinates give the rotary encoding the measured ray angle of each patch, evaluated per view, and solid-angle weighting scales the point loss by the area each pixel covers on the sphere, so neither the encoding nor the supervision depends on the lens. Any3R is therefore a feed-forward model that takes any central camera, and is robust to mixing many camera types per scene, with no retraining per lens. On a benchmark of thirteen central cameras across three datasets, where every method reconstructs with its own predicted camera poses, it is the most consistently accurate method. Any3R enables scalable 3D reconstruction from heterogeneous crowdsourced and archival imagery collections sourced across diverse camera types. Project page: this https URL

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Multimodal intelligence can be viewed as human-guided co-evolution between worlds and agents through interaction. However, scaling this process faces two bottlenecks: constructing heterogeneous, verifiable environments is costly, and manual Harness iteration cannot keep pace with growing task diversity and complexity. A key insight is that tasks with different modalities and surface forms can share the same underlying capability requirements. We therefore introduce The Trinity, a recursive self-improvement framework coupling world generation with Harness evolution through three roles: Creator, Explorer, and Evolver. Drawing on verified execution trajectories, Evolver shifts the basis of task organization from surface categories to shared requirements, reorganizing the original task tree into a hierarchical capability tree. We introduce Capability-Driven Real2Sim2Real: for heterogeneous tasks at the same node, Creator abstracts their common requirements into simplified, verifiable simulated worlds for capability learning, while evaluation on original tasks measures transfer. Along this capability tree, Explorer progressively evolves reusable capability modules through selective inheritance, forming a Harness tree whose validated updates accumulate across iterations within a fixed topology. The resulting Harnesses specialize in different capabilities, motivating a Capability-Aware Harness Router that uses public task information to select compatible module configurations from the tree. Our evolved Harnesses improve mean performance by 34 points across 28 training benchmarks and 26 points across 18 unseen benchmarks, including two real-world embodied tasks.

145. 【2610.36022】Learning and Reasoning with Geometry: How Generation Helps Spatial Understanding

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36022

作者：Rui Yang,Yanwei Li,Sili Chen,Jingjia Huang,Yi Lin,Hengshuang Zhao

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：produce spatially consistent, Image and video, understanding remains underexplored, consistent visual content, spatially consistent visual

备注： demo: [this https URL](https://yangr116.github.io/geoug-project-page)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Image and video generators can produce spatially consistent visual content, but how to use this capability to improve spatial understanding remains underexplored. We study this problem from two perspectives: geometric supervision during training and explicit geometric evidence during inference. To this end, we introduce a unified geometric understanding and generation model (GeoUG) that integrates depth and multi-view generation with spatial understanding. First, we design a multi-scale depth codec that enables metric-depth generation using a pretrained visual autoencoder, and show that joint training yields task-dependent gains in spatial understanding. Second, we introduce 3DCueBench to control geometric difficulty while balancing selected 2D cues, and find that generated depth provides larger reasoning gains on questions requiring fine-grained geometric discrimination. Building on this finding, we develop adaptive interleaved reasoning, in which GeoUG first drafts an answer and then decides whether to generate geometric evidence to confirm or revise it. We train this decision through reinforcement learning, balancing accuracy gains over retaining the draft against generation cost. GeoUG achieves circular accuracies of 97.9%, 90.8%, and 94.2% on CV-Bench-3D, 3DCueBench, and DA-2K with fewer generation calls than always generating.

146. 【2610.36013】OmniBrain-0: A Unified Agentic Model for Embodied Intelligence

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36013

作者：Xiaomi Embodied Intelligence Team:Yuechen Luo,Chong He,Fang Li,Shaoqing Xu,Jingkai Wang,Menglun Su,Jiaxin Liu,Shengyin Jiang,Zhiyi Lai,Long Chen,Longlong Wang,Liang Hu,Haochen Liu,Yuncheng Jiang,Hongwei Xie,Jieming Cui,Hanbing Li,Junjie Huang,Zhixiang Duan,Hexin Zhang,Weixiang Liang,Ying Gong,Yuhan Wang,Yong Pan,Pengna Li,Kangyi Wu,Ken Deng,Kun Ma,Fuxi Wen,Zhi-xin Yang,Hangjun Ye(University of Macau, Tsinghua University)

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：General-purpose embodied intelligence, intelligence requires robots, coordinate diverse skills, embodied intelligence requires, General-purpose embodied

备注： Technical Report; 40 pages, 27 figures, 8 tables; Project Page at [this https URL](https://public-bots.github.io/OmniBrain-0/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:General-purpose embodied intelligence requires robots to coordinate diverse skills over long horizons, track task progress, and recover from failures. Specialist policies alone do not ensure task-level reliability, while powerful multimodal agents rely on costly reasoning and external experience that compact models struggle to reproduce. We introduce OmniBrain-0, a lightweight unified embodied agent that coordinates manipulation and navigation through a shared high-level decision model, integrating task understanding, planning, progress tracking, and feedback-driven recovery. Our progressive training framework combines embodied pretraining, domain-specialized agent distillation, and multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD). It distills powerful teachers' interaction experience into manipulation and navigation experts, then consolidates their decision capabilities into OmniBrain-0. Unification therefore occurs within the learned decision model rather than only at the tool interface. We further introduce Rubric-Guided Recursive Self-Improvement (RSI), a data flywheel in which a Meta-Agent turns execution evidence into refined rubrics, step-level reminders, and verified trajectories for domain-expert updates before their reconsolidation into OmniBrain-0 through MOPD. We evaluate OmniBrain-0 on LIBERO-Pro, LIBERO-Plus, RoboCasa365, RoboTwin 2.0, and R2R-CE Val-Unseen, as well as multiple real-world manipulation tasks. OmniBrain-0 combines strong cross-domain task performance with substantially lower inference and execution overhead than its closed-source teacher, demonstrating efficient transfer and consolidation of embodied capabilities into a compact unified agent.

147. 【2610.35961】Knowledge distillation across teacher architectures for compact diabetic retinopathy grading

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35961

作者：Mossa Manisa,Habiba Jahan Priya,A.K.M. Fakhrul Hossain

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Diabetic retinopathy grading, retinal fundus photographs, Diabetic retinopathy, five-class diabetic retinopathy, retinopathy grading

备注： 17 pages, 3 figures, 18 tables. Submitted to PLOS ONE

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Diabetic retinopathy grading from retinal fundus photographs is important but difficult to deploy on resource-constrained hardware. We conducted a controlled study of offline response-based knowledge distillation for five-class diabetic retinopathy grading on APTOS 2019, comparing ResNet-50, MobileNetV3-Large, and Swin Transformer-Tiny (Swin-T) teachers distilled into a MobileNetV3-Small student. A 3 x 4 x 2 grid of teacher, temperature, and hard-label weight was screened at one seed, while six configurations and the no-distillation baseline were evaluated across three seeds. The highest observed single-seed quadratic weighted kappa was 0.9093 (macro-F1 0.6851) for Swin-T at temperature 4 and hard-label weight 0.7; the corresponding three-seed mean was 0.9024, compared with 0.8804 for the baseline. The student used 1.52 M parameters and 5.94 MB, representing an 18.07x parameter reduction relative to Swin-T. Temperature and hard-label weight effects were teacher- and configuration-dependent, while small performance differences were sensitive to random seed. Knowledge distillation produced a compact student with competitive performance under the evaluated protocol, but independent external validation remains necessary.

148. 【2610.35960】From Slices to Volumes: Comparing 2D, 2.5D, and 3D Residual Networks for Pulmonary Nodule Malignancy-Risk Classification

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35960

作者：Bahodir Nazarov,Zineb Gharib,Fatima Zazoul

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：pulmonary nodule malignancy-risk, nodule malignancy-risk classification, residual networks, networks for pulmonary, malignancy-risk classification

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Purpose: To compare 2D, 2.5D, and 3D residual networks for pulmonary nodule malignancy-risk classification and evaluate momentum-contrast pretraining, mild augmentation, and exclusion of indeterminate ratings. Methods: A retrospectively prepared LIDC-IDRI cohort contained 2,654 nodules labeled benign, indeterminate, or malignant using median radiologist ratings. Patient-disjoint splits comprised 1,876 training, 382 validation, and 396 test nodules. Representations included one axial slice, five neighboring slices, and a local CT volume. Each track evaluated a randomly initialized baseline, momentum-contrast pretraining followed by fine-tuning, and supervised training with mild augmentation. Checkpoints were selected by validation Macro-F1. The 2D experiments used three seeds; 2.5D and 3D results were single-run estimates. A separate binary experiment excluded indeterminate nodules. Results: Augmented models achieved test Macro-F1 values of $0.6134\pm0.0075$ for 2D, $0.6537$ for 2.5D, and $0.5403$ for 3D; the 2D uncertainty denotes population standard deviation across seeds. Corresponding accuracies were $0.6128$, $0.6869$, and $0.5455$. Augmentation improved Macro-F1 within every track, whereas the tested pretraining recipes reduced it. Binary Macro-F1 values were $0.8227$, $0.8664$, and $0.7554$, respectively. Conclusion: Augmented 2.5D achieved the highest observed performance. Differences in preprocessing, optimization, capacity, and replication limit attribution to dimensionality. Labels reflect radiologist-assessed malignancy risk rather than pathology-confirmed cancer. Code and experimental results are available at this https URL.

149. 【2610.35910】Physiognomy Revisited: How Vision-Language Models Encode Sensitive Attributes Through Image Transformation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35910

作者：Soumyajit Datta,Arka Dutta,Ashiqur R. KhudaBukhsh

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Computers and Society (cs.CY)

关键词：Emergent generative, generative AI technologies, failure modes, remarkably beneficial, suffer from unforeseen

备注： Accepted at EMNLP 2026 (Main Conference Track)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Emergent generative AI technologies, while remarkably beneficial across several tasks and domains, can suffer from unforeseen vulnerabilities and difficult-to-anticipate failure modes. Such failure modes can disparately affect vulnerable communities. This paper investigates two well-known vision language models (VLMs) and inspects how these models encode protected identities while generating images. We show that when VLMs are requested to transform human face images to reflect race identities (e.g., Hispanic, Black, White, or Asian) and sexual orientation (e.g., gay or straight), VLMs often comply with such requests. We demonstrate that compliance (and refusal) is non-deterministic, group-sensitive, and generated images are statistically distinguishable, often accentuating deep-seated stereotypes. We further show that representation biases introduced during image generation can re-emerge in textual reasoning and characterization tasks. Finally, we show that this worrisome finding is not limited to racial identity or sexual orientation, image generation can perpetuate bias around criminality, raising concerning possibilities of AI-assisted profiling.

150. 【2610.35905】ask-Utility IQA: Rethinking Image Quality Assessment in Embodied Tasks

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35905

作者：Jirong Zha,Yanwei Liao,Yemin Wang,Xiangmin Yi,Siqi Peng,Yingfeng Chen,Chen Gao,Xinlei Chen

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：existing methods learn, methods learn task-agnostic, underpins embodied imaging, generic human ratings, embodied imaging pipelines

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Image quality assessment (IQA) underpins embodied imaging pipelines by judging whether visual quality satisfies downstream tasks, yet most existing methods learn task-agnostic scores aligned with generic human ratings on static benchmarks. This objective mismatches embodied and interactive settings, where image adequacy depends on task goals, context, and action requirements that shape an agent's decisions. We argue that IQA should shift from score regression to goal-conditioned judgment defined by embodied task utility. To this end, we propose an MLLM-based embodied IQA agent framework that reasons about task requirements, grounds quality evidence, and produces decision-facing assessments. A compact VisDrone evidence study shows that conventional perceptual metrics and generic MLLM-based IQA scores are weak proxies for downstream VLM task success, while a task-conditioned utility score better predicts whether visual input supports the target task. These findings support a task-utility-oriented reframing of IQA for embodied, explainable, and decision-aware visual perception.

151. 【2610.35904】ObjectTwin: Measuring the Asset Correspondence Gap for Capture-Anchored Digital Twins

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35904

作者：Zhenghe Guo

类目：Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：Object-wise reconstruction increasingly, reconstruction increasingly inserts, Object-wise reconstruction, increasingly inserts generated, inserts generated meshes

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Object-wise reconstruction increasingly inserts generated meshes into captured scenes as editable proxies for observed objects. This use creates a requirement absent from isolated-asset scores and pose estimates: at the captured metric state, the proxy should reproduce calibrated observations of the same instance. We present ObjectTwin, a scan-grounded measurement protocol that freezes the instance, cameras, scan-projected support and recorded depth, metric anchor, and evaluation denominator, then replaces only the object representation. The resulting asset correspondence gap measures lost observation agreement relative to scan-derived visible geometry without treating the scan as complete physical ground truth. Across two indoor RGB-D capture domains and two generation backends, visible, anchored generated assets lose 0.302-0.497 MaskIoU under this replacement. Every tested scene-level estimate is positive, while same-input generator preference reverses across capture domains. Coverage and overlap-depth analyses locate disagreement in both visible support and surface placement; the evaluated conservative pose refinement does not systematically recover it. These results establish capture correspondence as a separately measurable interface between generated-asset quality and stronger physical or task-level evaluation.

152. 【2610.35871】Real-Time Smart Retail Analytics through Fog Computing using the RetailLightNet Lightweight Model

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35871

作者：Mayank Atreya,Navin Chhibber,Shilpa Ajay,R Loshini,K Anitha

类目：Networking and Internet Architecture (cs.NI); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Smart retail systems now depend on the application of real-time video analytics, which can track customer behavior, manage shelf inventory, and enhance in-store processes. Currently cloud-based applications of video analytics face multiple challenges such as high latency of the communication itself, privacy concerns surrounding transmitting raw video, and the computational demands of the heavy vision models used on the often limited network infrastructure. These challenges lead to delays in decision making where prompt action is crucial. To address these short comings, this research proposes Fog-based analytics withRetailLightNet (F RLN), an ultra lightweight deep learning model which is specifically suited for the edge, resource constrained hardware capabilities of devices like the NVIDIA Jetson Nano. The approach utilizes publicly available retail datasets, SKU110K, and RPC, which contains an assortment of product, shelf, and customer interaction scenarios for this http URL processes include preprocessing images through normalization and employing motion-gated frame selection to improve efficiency by omitting redundant frames to ease the workload on the fog processing. Internal within Retail LightNet, image feature extraction is realized through depth wise separable convolutions which help support spatial and channel-wise learning without heavy computational costs. The model employs two prediction heads presence classification, and density regression, supporting shelf monitoring and queue assessment. The proposed F RLN framework attained better results in terms of precision (0.988), recall (0.976), and F1-score(0.976) when compared with existing convolutional neural network. Keywords: fog computing, motion-gated frame selection,NVIDIA jetson, retail lightnet, smart retail.

153. 【2610.37213】Retrospective macromolecular pool fraction mapping from conventional clinical MRI: a physics-guided self-supervised deep learning framework

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37213

作者：Jelmer van Lune,Miha Fuderer,Oscar van der Heide,Matteo Maspero,Martin B. Schilder,Jan Willem Dankbaar,Stefano Mandija,Alessandro Sbrizzi

类目：Medical Physics (physics.med-ph); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：Macromolecular pool fraction, tissue macromolecular content, reconstruction methods limit, macromolecular content, Macromolecular pool

备注： 38 pages, 24 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Macromolecular pool fraction (MPF) is a quantitative imaging biomarker sensitive to myelin integrity and other changes in tissue macromolecular content, but specialized acquisitions and reconstruction methods limit its use in large-scale studies. We present a physics-guided, self-supervised deep learning framework for retrospectively estimating MPF directly from conventional clinical brain MRI ($T_1$-weighted, $T_2$-weighted, and fluid-attenuated inversion recovery [FLAIR]) without paired quantitative reference scans. The framework embeds an analytical two-pool magnetization transfer signal model that related sequence-averaged radiofrequency transmit power to apparent longitudinal relaxation. To accommodate heterogeneous clinical protocols, the network conditions intermediate features on acquisition parameters via adaptive instance normalization. It was trained and evaluated on a multisite, multivendor, multiprotocol dataset of 7,705 MRI sessions (3 T). Across 1,237 test sessions, mean MPF was $0.182 \pm 0.007$ in white matter and $0.144 \pm 0.009$ in gray matter, with limited variation across vendor-by-protocol groups (between-group coefficient of variation: 2.65% and 5.63%, respectively). In 70 sessions with demyelinating-disease labels, MPF was 35.6% lower in segmented lesions than in normal-appearing white matter. MPF maps from two different clinical protocols acquired in a healthy volunteer showed a mean voxel-wise absolute percentage difference of 7.7% and a concordance correlation coefficient of $0.888$, indicating good cross-protocol consistency. These results suggest that magnetization transfer-informed self-supervised learning enables stable MPF mapping from conventional clinical MRI, providing a scalable approach to investigate demyelinating pathology in both existing imaging archives and prospectively acquired conventional clinical scans without dedicated qMT acquisitions.

154. 【2610.37056】Quantized Edge Inference of a Self-Supervised SAR-Optical Encoder on Commodity x86 Hardware

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37056

作者：Christian Sanchez-Leon,Dongming Chen

类目：Image and Video Processing (eess.IV); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：Self-supervised vision transformers, Earth observation, Self-supervised vision, CPU, rarely characterized

备注： 3 pages, 1 figure, 3 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Self-supervised vision transformers are increasingly used as reusable encoders for Earth observation, but their inference cost on the hardware available in the field is rarely characterized. We deploy a pretrained SAR-optical masked-autoencoder encoder on three commodity x86 nodes, from a low-power CPU without AVX2 to an AVX2+VNNI reference, at FP32, FP16 and INT8 on the CPU and, where present, the integrated GPU, and report latency, energy (RAPL and wall plug) and linear-probe accuracy. Quantization is accuracy-neutral: INT8 changes micro-mAP by only +0.0006, within the seed spread, so precision becomes a pure cost decision. INT8 lowers CPU latency on every node, by 1.45x even on the CPU without AVX2, and CPU energy wherever it is measured, and the VNNI reference (1.53x) suggests a further gain beyond AVX2. The Pareto-optimal device shifts from the integrated GPU, which is 4.38x faster than the CPU on the weakest node, to the CPU as the CPU class improves.

155. 【2610.36646】Image Modeling: From Handcrafted Priors to Foundation Image Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36646

作者：A. Murat Tekalp

类目：Image and Video Processing (eess.IV); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：explicit probabilistic characterization, data-driven deep foundation, inverse problems, solve inverse problems, Bayesian framework

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:This paper unifies six decades of image modeling, evolving from handcrafted mathematical tractable priors to solve inverse problems in imaging to data-driven deep foundation image models for both image generation and solving inverse problems, under a common Bayesian framework that formulates image modeling as an implicit or explicit probabilistic characterization of a data manifold. Every image restoration or generative task fundamentally seeks to answer the same question: What makes an image look like a real image? In the Bayesian framework, this question is encapsulated by the prior probability density p(x), or its more optimization-friendly counterpart, the log-prior log p(x). While early works in solving inverse problems, such as Tikhonov, Total Variation (TV), and Non-local priors relied on explicit handcrafted definitions of log prior to model local smoothness, modern techniques, such as Plug-and-Play (PnP) priors and Denoising Diffusion models, are rooted in algorithmic uncoupling and implicit manifold learning that apply to both inverse problems and conditional image generation. By grounding imaging inverse problems and conditional image generation under a single mathematical engine: the gradient of the log-prior, or the Stein Score Function, we bridge the gap between signal processing and machine learning communities, turning what seems like parallel developments into a single, evolution of image modeling from handcrafted analytical models to models learned from big data, demonstrating that the pursuit of the denoising score is the common thread connecting variational optimization (in MAP estimation) to posterior sampling (in modern foundation image models).

156. 【2610.36364】When Slides Met Scans: Cross-Attention for Renal Cancer Prognosis

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36364

作者：Anusha Krishnamoorthy,Basudha Pal

类目：Image and Video Processing (eess.IV); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：provide complementary views, Histopathology and computed, computed tomography, provide complementary, renal cancer

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Histopathology and computed tomography (CT) provide complementary views of renal cancer. We connect these modalities through bidirectional cross-attention between 16 histology prototypes and up to 32 kidney-centered CT tokens from frozen foundation models. In 210 TCGA-KIRC patients with 46 disease-specific survival (DSS) events, 5-fold evaluation yielded a C-index of $0.760\pm0.081$ and 3-year AUROC of 0.773 (95\% CI 0.678--0.860), the highest averages among evaluated unimodal and fusion baselines. External testing in 180 CPTAC-CCRCC patients yielded DSS C-index of 0.629 with CT withheld. Attribution analysis linked predictions to image regions while exposing acquisition artifacts. These findings support further study of cross-scale fusion and independent multimodal validation.
